### [1] [RynnWorld-4D: 4D Embodied World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06559v1)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06559v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.06559v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=RynnWorld-4D%3A+4D+Embodied+World+Models+for+Robotic+Manipulation++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出基于RGB-DF的4D世界模型及逆动力学策略头，在双手灵巧操作任务上达到SOTA。

研究问题: 现有2D像素视频表示无法充分捕获场景的4D动态信息，导致世界预测与机器人策略学习之间存在表示鸿沟，难以直接为底层末端执行器动作提供有效指导。

主要创新点: 提出RGB-DF多模态协同表示作为物理基础的4D动态表征；设计三分支扩散架构（跨模态注意力+逐帧3D RoPE）统一生成RGB、深度和光流；提出逆动力学头在单次前向传播中绕过多步去噪直接输出动作。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 开放世界机器人操作需要理解场景的3D结构如何随交互运动，而RGB-DF将外观、几何与时间运动对齐，比2D表示更接近末端执行器动作空间，有助于缩小世界预测与策略学习的差距。

主要方法: 提出RynnWorld-4D生成模型，采用三分支架构结合跨模态注意力与逐帧3D RoPE，在统一扩散过程中从单张RGB-D图像和语言指令联合生成未来RGB帧、深度图和光流。构建含2.544亿帧的Rynn4DDataset 1.0数据集（含深度和光流伪标签）。进一步设计RynnWorld-4D-Policy逆动力学头，单次前向传播利用内部4D表示闭环输出机器人动作。

实验 / 验证: 评估了RynnWorld-4D的4D预测质量（时间与空间连贯性），并在真实世界灵巧双手操作任务上测试RynnWorld-4D-Policy的性能。

主要结果: RynnWorld-4D产生时空连贯的4D预测；RynnWorld-4D-Policy在真实世界灵巧双手操作任务上达到SOTA，尤其在需要空间精度和时间协调的任务中表现优异。

结论: RGB-DF多模态表示有效桥接世界预测与策略学习，所提出的4D世界模型及逆动力学策略在双手灵巧操作中表现出色。

局限 / 后续问题: 深度和光流标签为伪标签，可能引入噪声；实验仅验证于双手灵巧操作任务，泛化能力待进一步验证。

中文摘要: 开放世界中的机器人操作不仅需要识别场景的外观，还需要预判其3D结构在交互中如何运动。我们认为，同步的RGB、深度和光流（即RGB-DF）提供了一种物理基础表示，能够捕获场景潜在的4D动态。与2D像素视频相比，这种多模态协同将视觉外观与几何结构及时间运动对齐，构建了一个显著更接近机器人系统所需的底层末端执行器动作的表示空间，从而缩小了世界预测与策略学习之间的差距。基于这一洞察，我们提出了RynnWorld-4D，一个生成模型，能够在统一的扩散过程中，根据单张RGB-D图像和语言指令同时生成未来的RGB帧、深度图和光流。该4D世界模型采用三分支架构，将跨模态注意力与逐帧3D RoPE相结合，确保外观、几何和运动的一致演化。为提供大规模训练数据，我们构建了Rynn4DDataset 1.0，一个包含超过2.544亿帧的大规模数据集，涵盖自我中心视角的人类视频和机器人操作视频，并配有高质量的深度和光流伪标签。我们进一步提出了RynnWorld-4D-Policy，一个逆动力学头，在单次前向传播中利用RynnWorld-4D的内部4D表示，绕过昂贵的多步去噪过程，以闭环方式输出机器人动作。实验表明，RynnWorld-4D能够产生时间和空间上连贯的4D预测，并且RynnWorld-4D-Policy在真实世界灵巧双手操作任务上达到了最先进的性能，尤其在需要空间精度和时间协调的任务中表现突出。

英文原始摘要: Robotic manipulation in the open world requires not only recognizing what a scene looks like, but also anticipating how its 3D structure moves under interaction. We argue that synchronized RGB, depth, and optical flow, namely RGB-DF, provide a physically grounded representation that captures the underlying 4D dynamics of a scene. Compared to 2D pixel videos, this multi-modal synergy aligns visual appearance with geometric structure and temporal motion, creating a representation space significantly closer to the low-level end-effector actions demanded by robotic systems, thereby narrowing the gap between world prediction and policy learning. Building on this insight, we introduce RynnWorld-4D, a generative model that co-produces future RGB frames, depth maps, and optical flow from a single RGB-D image and a language instruction within one unified diffusion process. This 4D world model features a tri-branch architecture that integrates cross-modal attention with frame-wise 3D RoPE, ensuring that appearance, geometry, and motion evolve consistently. To supply training data at scale, we curate Rynn4DDataset 1.0, a massive dataset of over 254.4 million frames across egocentric human and robotic manipulation videos with high-quality pseudo-labels for depth and optical flow. We further propose RynnWorld-4D-Policy, an inverse dynamics head that consumes the internal 4D representations of RynnWorld-4D in a single forward pass, bypassing expensive multi-step denoising, to output robot actions in a closed-loop manner. Experiments show that RynnWorld-4D produces temporally and spatially coherent 4D predictions, and that RynnWorld-4D-Policy achieves state-of-the-art performance on real-world dexterous bimanual manipulation tasks, particularly excelling in tasks demanding spatial precision and temporal coordination.

</details>


### [2] [Neural-ESO: A Dual-Pathway Architecture for Provably Robust Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06535v1)
*Fan Zhang,Richie Suganda,Jinfeng Chen,Wenhua Liu,Hantao Fu,Bin Hu,Qin Lin*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06535v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.06535v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Neural-ESO%3A+A+Dual-Pathway+Architecture+for+Provably+Robust+Learning-Based+Control++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出神经扩展状态观测器双通路框架，兼顾神经网络前馈预测与传统ESO误差校正，实现扰动抑制的精度与鲁棒性平衡。

研究问题: 现有基于学习的控制方法在部署后过度依赖学习模型，缺乏对预测误差的纠正机制，难以在分布外场景下保持鲁棒性。

主要创新点: 双通路架构设计：神经网络前馈预测通路加速收敛，传统ESO校正通路补偿预测误差并防止对神经组件的过度依赖；通过对学习组件施加Lipschitz界约束保证闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 学习型控制方法在部署后过度依赖学习模型，在分布外场景下可靠性不足，需要在保持学习加速优势的同时引入纠正机制以增强鲁棒性。

主要方法: 设计Neural-ESO双通路架构，预测通路用神经网络提供前馈扰动估计，校正通路用传统ESO补偿预测误差；利用Lyapunov理论和小增益分析证明对学习组件施加Lipschitz界可保证闭环误差动力学的最终一致有界性。

实验 / 验证: 在受强地效扰动影响的四旋翼着陆任务上验证，涵盖正常分布和分布外场景，与最先进基线方法进行对比。

主要结果: 在训练、部署和迁移阶段均展现出更高的运行可靠性，展示了精度与鲁棒性之间的良好权衡，优于最先进的基线方法。

结论: Neural-ESO双通路框架有效平衡了学习型控制的精度优势与传统ESO的鲁棒性，在正常和分布外场景下均实现可靠的扰动抑制。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 本快报提出了一种基于神经扩展状态观测器（Neural-ESO）的学习使能扰动抑制框架。与现有的基于学习的控制方法在部署后很大程度上依赖所学模型不同，Neural-ESO采用双通路架构：预测通路使用神经网络提供前馈扰动估计以加速收敛，校正通路采用传统ESO来补偿预测误差并防止对神经组件的过度依赖。利用Lyapunov理论和小增益分析，我们证明了对学习组件施加Lipschitz界约束可保证闭环误差动力学的最终一致有界性。所提框架在受强地效扰动影响的四旋翼着陆任务上进行了验证，涵盖正常分布和分布外场景，展示了精度-鲁棒性权衡，并在训练、部署和迁移阶段相比最先进的基线方法展现出更高的运行可靠性。

英文原始摘要: A learning-enabled disturbance-rejection framework based on a Neural Extended State Observer (Neural-ESO) is presented in this letter. Unlike existing learning-based control methods that largely rely on the learned model once deployed, Neural-ESO adopts a dual-pathway architecture: a predictive pathway uses a neural network to provide a feedforward disturbance estimate that accelerates convergence, while a corrective pathway employs a conventional ESO to compensate prediction errors and prevent over-reliance on the neural component. Using Lyapunov theory and a small-gain analysis, we show that enforcing a Lipschitz bound on the learning component guarantees uniform ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop error dynamics. The proposed framework is validated on a quadrotor landing task subject to strong ground-effect disturbances across normal and out-of-distribution scenarios, demonstrating accuracy-robustness trade-off and greater operational reliability during training, deployment, and transfer compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [3] [The Large Cancer Assistant (LCA): A Model-Agnostic Orchestration Framework for Scalable Clinical Decision Support in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06531v1)
*Ghassen Marrakchi,Basarab Matei*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06531v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.06531v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=The+Large+Cancer+Assistant+%28LCA%29%3A+A+Model-Agnostic+Orchestration+Framework+for+Scalable+Clinical+Decision+Support+in+Oncology++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出LCA编排框架，通过算法不可渗透性将多模态数据接入与AI推理解耦，实现肿瘤临床决策的可扩展模块化支持。

研究问题: 肿瘤学多模态深度学习模型采用单体式设计，将数据接入、临床路由与AI推理刚性耦合，导致系统缺乏灵活性和可扩展性，难以适应临床决策支持的需求。

主要创新点: 提出基于算法不可渗透性原则的七元组架构LCA框架，通过入口理论利用几何深度学习标准化多模态数据，并以标准化中间载荷（SIP）建立架构边界，实现编排逻辑与黑盒AI模型的严格解耦。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 现有肿瘤学多模态AI模型的设计将数据接入、临床路由和推理流程刚性绑定，限制了系统在不稳定医院IT环境中的适应性和模块化扩展能力，需要一种灵活的编排框架来解决这些耦合问题。

主要方法: 将LCA形式化为七元组架构，核心包括：基于几何深度学习的入口理论进行多模态数据标准化；癌症切换模块实现动态数据编排；输出标准化中间载荷（SIP）将AI执行与医院IT基础设施隔离，确保编排逻辑与AI模型独立。

实验 / 验证: 在四个技术场景中进行了概念验证（PoC）实验，测试标称流程执行、AI模型替换下的路由投影不变性、数据异常注入下的补充数据请求召回率，以及多协议执行能力。

主要结果: 框架以可忽略的开销完成标称流程；AI模型替换时路由投影保持不变，验证了算法不可渗透性；数据异常注入下生成定向SDR的召回率达100%，验证了故障安全性；多协议执行能力亦获成功验证。

结论: LCA通过结构化解耦多模态接入与特征推理，提供了高度适应和模块化的编排基础；SIP建立的清晰架构边界为下游电子病历互操作性奠定了基础，可作为独立的未来范式。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 目的：肿瘤学中的多模态深度学习模型目前受限于单体式设计，即将数据接入、临床路由和人工智能（AI）推理刚性耦合。为解决这一不灵活性，我们提出了大型癌症助手（LCA），一个与模型无关的事后编排框架，旨在实现可扩展的临床决策支持。方法：LCA在数学上被形式化为基于算法不可渗透性原则的七元组架构，确保编排逻辑与底层黑盒AI模型严格独立。我们引入了入口理论，利用几何深度学习（GDL）沿不同的结构和医学轴标准化多模态患者数据。系统通过癌症切换模块动态编排数据，并通过输出标准化中间载荷（SIP）有意将核心AI执行与不稳定的医院IT基础设施隔离。结果：概念验证（PoC）在四个技术场景中验证了编排逻辑。该框架以可忽略的编排开销执行了标称流程。它通过在AI模型替换期间保持不变的路由投影，经验性地证明了算法不可渗透性，并通过在注入数据异常下实现100%召回率生成定向补充数据请求（SDR），验证了严格的故障安全性。多协议执行能力也得到了成功验证。结论：通过在结构上将多模态接入与特征推理解耦，LCA提供了一个高度适应性和模块化的编排基础。SIP建立了清晰的架构边界，天然地为下游电子病历（EMR）互操作性作为独立未来范式奠定了基础。

英文原始摘要: - Objective: Multimodal deep learning models in oncology are currently limited by monolithic designs that rigidly couple data ingestion, clinical routing, and artificial intelligence (AI) inference. To address this inflexibility, we propose the Large Cancer Assistant (LCA), a model-agnostic, post-hoc orchestration framework designed for scalable clinical decision support. - Methods: The LCA is mathematically formalized as a 7-tuple architecture grounded in the principle of Algorithmic Impermeability, ensuring the orchestration logic remains strictly independent of underlying black-box AI models. We introduce the Entry Theory, leveraging Geometric Deep Learning (GDL) to standardize multimodal patient data along distinct structural and medical axes. The system dynamically orchestrates data via a Cancer Switching Module and intentionally isolates the core AI execution from volatile hospital IT infrastructures by outputting a Standardized Intermediate Payload (SIP). - Results: A Proof of Concept (PoC) validated the orchestration logic across four technical scenarios. The framework executed a nominal flow with negligible orchestration overhead. It empirically demonstrated algorithmic impermeability by maintaining an invariant routing projection during AI model swaps, and it validated strict failure-safety by achieving a 100\% recall rate in generating targeted Supplementary Data Requests (SDR) under injected data anomalies. Multi-protocol execution capability was also successfully verified. - Conclusion: By structurally decoupling multimodal ingestion from feature inference, the LCA provides a highly adaptable and modular orchestration foundation. The SIP establishes a clear architectural boundary, natively setting the stage for downstream Electronic Medical Record (EMR) interoperability as an independent future paradigm.

</details>


### [4] [A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Elastodynamic Wave Propagation in Bimaterial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06479v1)
*Sonal Ankush Chibire,Jenn-Terng Gau,Bo Zhang*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06479v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.06479v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=A+Physics-Informed+Neural+Network+Framework+for+Elastodynamic+Wave+Propagation+in+Bimaterial+Systems++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出基于PINN的框架用于双材料瞬态弹性动力学波传播建模，精度高且无需额外有限元仿真即可泛化预测。

研究问题: 双材料系统中瞬态弹性动力学波传播的高保真建模依赖于昂贵的有限元仿真，缺乏一种兼顾精度与计算效率且可泛化至新材料属性和新时间点的连续代理模型。

主要创新点: 将轴对称线弹性方程及初始、边界、界面条件通过物理信息损失函数直接嵌入神经网络，并结合显式有限元仿真作为补充数据约束，构建连续的弹性动力学代理模型。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: PINN可将物理规律嵌入学习过程，有望替代或补充高成本有限元仿真，实现双材料弹性波传播的高效建模与泛化预测，服务高应变率固体力学和冲击工程。

主要方法: 构建基于PINN的轴对称线弹性动力学模型，以钢-铝分离式霍普金森压杆试样为对象，将控制方程、初始/边界/界面条件纳入物理信息损失函数，并利用ANSYS显式动力学有限元结果进行验证与训练数据约束。

实验 / 验证: 以钢-铝双材料试样为算例，使用有限元仿真验证预测精度；进行网格敏感性研究验证鲁棒性；对额外材料组合进行测试以验证方法通用性。

主要结果: 框架准确预测了界面波透射与反射，位移历程、面平均响应及主导应力应变演化均与有限元解高度吻合；网络可在未见过的时间点和修改的材料属性下预测波响应，无需额外仿真。

结论: 将PINN与显式有限元分析相结合，为非均匀固体弹性动力学波传播提供了准确且计算高效的框架，是一种有效的高应变率固体力学和冲击工程代理建模方法。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）为求解偏微分方程提供了一个有前景的框架，能够将底层物理规律直接嵌入学习过程。本研究提出了一个基于PINN的框架，用于对由轴对称线弹性方程控制的双材料系统中的瞬态弹性动力学波传播进行建模。研究考虑了一个代表分离式霍普金森压杆构型的钢-铝试样，并将控制性弹性动力学方程以及相应的初始条件、边界条件和界面条件通过物理信息损失函数直接嵌入网络中。使用ANSYS Workbench显式动力学进行的高保真有限元仿真被用于验证，并在训练过程中作为补充数据约束。所提出的框架准确预测了双材料界面的波透射与反射，并重现了轴向和径向位移历程、面平均响应以及主导应力与应变演化，与有限元解高度吻合。训练后的网络进一步展示了在先前未见过的时间点以及修改后的材料属性下预测波响应的能力，且无需额外的有限元仿真，从而为弹性动力学分析提供了一个连续的代理模型。网格敏感性研究证实了数值鲁棒性，而额外的材料组合则证明了所提方法的通用性。结果表明，将物理信息神经网络与显式有限元分析相结合，为非均匀固体中的弹性动力学波传播提供了一个准确且计算高效的框架，为高应变率固体力学和冲击工程应用提供了一种有效的代理建模方法。

英文原始摘要: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a promising framework for solving partial differential equations while embedding the underlying physical laws directly into the learning process. This study presents a PINN-based framework for modeling transient elastodynamic wave propagation in bimaterial systems governed by the axisymmetric equations of linear elasticity. A steel-aluminum specimen representative of a Split Hopkinson Pressure Bar configuration is considered, and the governing elastodynamic equations, together with the corresponding initial, boundary, and interface conditions, are incorporated directly into the network through a physics-informed loss function. High-fidelity finite-element simulations performed using ANSYS Workbench Explicit Dynamics are used for validation and as supplementary data constraints during training. The proposed framework accurately predicts wave transmission and reflection across the bimaterial interface and reproduces axial and radial displacement histories, face-averaged responses, and the dominant stress and strain evolution with close agreement to the finite-element solutions. The trained network further demonstrates the ability to predict wave responses at previously unseen time instants and for modified material properties without requiring additional finite-element simulations, providing a continuous surrogate model for elastodynamic analysis. Mesh-sensitivity studies confirm numerical robustness, while additional material combinations demonstrate the generality of the proposed methodology. The results show that integrating physics-informed neural networks with explicit finite-element analysis provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for elastodynamic wave propagation in heterogeneous solids, offering an effective surrogate modeling approach for high-rate solid mechanics and impact engineering applications.

Estimating $R_t$, typically from case reports or hospitalization counts, poses a challenging inverse problem. One key issue is lag: $R_t$ acts at the moment of transmission, while the data it generates surface days later. To handle this delay and infer recent infections in real time, popular methods take a Bayesian approach, which can be slow and sensitive to prior specification. As an alternative, we propose ConvRt, a frequentist method for retrospective and real-time estimation. ConvRt deconvolves latent infections and then estimates $R_t$ with successive penalized-likelihood steps, using a spline basis to model smooth curves. Across both stylized and data-driven simulations, we demonstrate favorable performance in point estimation, uncertainty quantification, and runtime. Moreover, by untangling smoothness from future projections, ConvRt enables researchers to assess which qualitative narratives about $R_t$ the data support.

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论研究，未涉及实际数值实验；通过复杂性理论论证而非实证实验验证了学习分离。

结论: 在基于哈密顿量演化的自然机器学习任务上实现了严格的学习分离，建立了量子学习理论、量子模拟与QML之间的联系，并在学习辅助认证量子模拟中给出应用解释。

中文摘要: 鉴于量子计算机天然适合模拟量子多体系统的行为，一个自然的问题随之产生：能否构造具有物理动机的量子机器学习（QML）任务，使其展现出学习分离？我们通过可能近似正确（PAC）学习的视角研究了量子多体动力学的可学习性来回答这一问题。具体而言，我们设计了一个监督学习问题，其训练集由随机化稳定子探针态的描述、从多项式大小的时间区间[0,T]中均匀采样的演化时间、以及在未知哈密顿量下演化后状态上对某些可观测量计算的期望值组成。针对此学习任务，我们提供了一个高效的量子过程：其训练阶段从短时间训练样本中学习底层哈密顿量，其部署阶段将哈密顿量模拟与经典阴影协议相结合，对新的数据点进行推断。相比之下，O(poly(n))时间实例的存在性确保了经典困难性：通过将BQP完全计算嵌入到Feynman-Kitaev时钟哈密顿量构造的低交叉变体的多项式长时间动力学中，我们证明，对于某类输入分布，不存在随机经典多项式时间算法能够满足我们的学习条件，除非BQP⊆P/poly。此外，我们证明该经典困难实例仍保持量子可学习性。我们还给出了结果在学习辅助认证量子模拟中的解释。综合来看，我们的结果展示了基于哈密顿量演化的自然机器学习任务的严格学习分离，同时建立了量子学习理论、量子模拟与QML之间的联系。

英文原始摘要: Given that quantum computers are naturally suited to simulate the behavior of quantum many-body systems, an immediate question arises: can one formulate physically motivated quantum machine learning (QML) tasks that exhibit learning separations? We address this problem by studying the learnability of quantum many-body dynamics from the perspective of probably approximately correct (PAC)-learning. Concretely, we devise a supervised learning problem where the training set consists of specifications of randomized stabilizer probe states, evolution times sampled uniformly from a polynomially large time interval $[0,T]$, coupled with expectation values of certain observables evaluated on the resulting time-evolved state under an unknown Hamiltonian. For this learning task, we provide an efficient quantum procedure whose training phase learns the underlying Hamiltonian from short-time training samples, and whose deployment phase combines Hamiltonian simulation with the classical shadows protocol to perform inference on a newly given data point. By contrast, the existence of $O(\mathsf{poly}(n))$-time instances ensures classical hardness: by embedding a $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete computation into the polynomially long time-dynamics of a low-intersection variant of the Feynman-Kitaev clock Hamiltonian construction, we show that, for a certain family of input distributions, no randomized classical polynomial-time algorithm can fulfill our learning condition, unless $\mathsf{BQP}\subseteq\mathsf{P/poly}$. Furthermore, we show that the classically hard instance maintains quantum learnability. We also give an interpretation of our results in learning-assisted certified quantum simulation. Taken together, our results demonstrate a rigorous learning separation for a natural ML task based on Hamiltonian evolution, while building connections between quantum learning theory, quantum simulation, and QML.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 电力系统发电机间存在动态耦合关系，现有方法要么使用固定拓扑忽略状态依赖性，要么忽视方向性因果影响，亟需同时捕捉两类耦合特征的框架。

主要方法: 从测量数据推断动态结构图，通过非线性因果发现获取方向性因果图，利用端到端学习的逐边融合机制对两种表示自适应加权，再通过GRU传播融合后的图以预测故障后轨迹。

主要结果: C-DNR相较动态结构基线将自回归预测误差降低73%；在因果方法中仅PCMCI取得一致改进，因其能从振荡相关性中分离方向性依赖；融合权重与电气拓扑一致，提供可解释诊断。

结论: C-DNR通过融合动态结构图与因果图有效提升了暂态稳定预测精度，同时融合权重提供了与电气拓扑对齐的透明可解释诊断。

局限 / 后续问题: 评估仅在IEEE 39节点系统上进行，泛化性尚需验证；在多种因果发现方法中仅PCMCI有效，其他方法的适用性有待进一步探索。

中文摘要: 电力系统由动态耦合的发电机组成，这促使了图神经网络（GNN）在在线暂态稳定预测中的应用。传统GNN框架通常受限于固定的基于导纳的拓扑结构，无法捕捉状态相关的耦合，或受限于忽视方向性影响的数据驱动方法。本文提出了因果动态网络表示（Causal Dynamic Network Representation, C-DNR），这是一种新颖的框架，在时序建模之前融合了发电机间交互的两种互补表示：从测量数据推断的动态结构图和通过非线性因果发现获得的方向性因果图。一种端到端学习的逐边融合机制为每对发电机自适应地加权这些表示，所得图通过门控循环单元（GRU）传播以预测故障后轨迹。在IEEE 39节点系统上的评估表明，与动态结构基线相比，C-DNR将自回归预测误差降低了73%。在所评估的因果方法中，仅Peter-Clark Momentary Conditional Independence（PCMCI）取得了一致的改进，这归因于其能够从误导性振荡相关性中分离出方向性依赖关系。学习到的融合权重进一步提供了与电气拓扑一致的可解释诊断，为预测过程提供了透明的成对洞察。

英文原始摘要: Power systems consist of dynamically coupled generators, motivating the use of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for online transient stability prediction. Traditional GNN frameworks are often constrained by fixed admittance-based topologies that fail to capture state-dependent coupling, or by data-driven methods that neglect directional influences. This paper proposes Causal Dynamic Network Representation (C-DNR), a novel framework that fuses two complementary representations of inter-generator interactions prior to temporal modeling: a dynamic structural graph inferred from measurements and a directional causal graph obtained via nonlinear causal discovery. An end-to-end learned edge-wise fusion mechanism adaptively weights these representations for each generator pair, and the resulting graph is propagated through a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) to predict post-fault trajectories. Evaluated on the IEEE 39-bus system, C-DNR reduces autoregressive prediction error by 73% compared to a dynamic structural baseline. Among the evaluated causal methods, only Peter--Clark Momentary Conditional Independence (PCMCI) achieves consistent improvements, owing to its ability to isolate directional dependencies from misleading oscillatory correlations. The learned fusion weights further provide interpretable diagnostics aligned with the electrical topology, offering transparent, pairwise insight into the prediction process.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

结论: 在线自适应辨识与离线神经算子近似相结合的方案能够为未知Volterra级数驱动的非线性双曲PDE提供实时可行的稳定自适应控制，理论保证完备。

中文摘要: 自适应控制在线学习被控对象，而神经算子控制离线学习控制增益。我们将二者结合起来，用于一类动力学由未知Volterra级数（含任意多核函数）支配的非线性双曲偏微分方程。基于观测器的无源标识器在线学习该级数的一个截断。从参数估计合成反步核函数的无穷维映射——即一个在维数递增的单纯形域上级联的PDE系统，实时求解不可行——由神经算子离线一次性近似。闭环系统随后串联承载两个学习过程：被控对象的在线学习馈入一个离线学习好的PDE求解器，其输出为在线控制增益。我们证明了被控对象状态、观测器状态和输入的闭环稳定性与渐近调节，且稳定域在神经算子精度提升时恢复到精确核函数对应的稳定域。我们用单个Lyapunov函数同时吸收三类扰动——截断无穷Volterra级数、在线辨识被控对象以及近似增益——且这些扰动均为渐近消失的。

英文原始摘要: Adaptive control learns the plant online; neural-operator control learns the control gains offline. We bring the two together for a class of nonlinear hyperbolic PDEs whose dynamics are governed by an unknown Volterra series of arbitrarily many kernels. An observer-based passive identifier learns a truncation of this series online. The infinite-dimensional map that synthesizes the backstepping kernels from the parameter estimates -- a cascade of PDEs on simplex domains of increasing dimension, prohibitive to solve in real time -- is approximated once, offline, by a neural operator. The closed loop then carries two learning processes in series: online learning of the plant feeds an offline-learned PDE solver, whose output is the online control gains. We prove closed-loop stability and asymptotic regulation of the plant state, observer state, and input, on a basin that recovers the exact-kernel basin as the neural-operator accuracy improves. With a single Lyapunov function we absorb at once the perturbations -- all vanishing -- of truncating an infinite Volterra series, of identifying the plant online, and of approximating the gains.

</details>

期刊:

一句话结论: 提出ICNDM框架，通过输入依赖的收缩正则化在学习时变控制输入的同时保证增量指数收敛。

研究问题: 现有神经微分方程在处理外部控制输入时，要么缺乏严格的结构保证（启发式处理），要么仅在恒定或消失输入的受限假设下才能保证稳定性，无法有效处理时变控制输入。

主要创新点: 提出ICNDM框架，通过输入依赖的收缩正则化将时变控制输入无缝融入模型，同时利用嵌入式输入编码器和参数化度量网络联合学习非自治向量场与广义黎曼收缩度量，并形式化建立了输入到状态收缩性质。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 数据驱动的动态系统学习需要能处理时变外部控制输入的连续时间模型，同时保证结构稳定性和收敛性，但现有方法无法同时满足这两个需求。

主要方法: 设计包含嵌入式输入编码器和参数化度量网络的架构，联合学习非自治神经向量场和广义黎曼收缩度量；推导输入依赖收缩的充分条件，在有界外部激励下形式化建立输入到状态收缩性质，并通过收缩正则化实现训练目标。

实验 / 验证: 在高度非线性混沌振荡器和永磁同步电机（PMSM）驱动系统的实验数据上进行数值评估，与最先进的神经微分方程基准方法进行对比。

主要结果: ICNDM在长期展开误差上实现大幅降低，且对输入扰动展现出优越的结构鲁棒性，显著优于现有基准方法。

结论: ICNDM成功弥合了时变控制输入处理与结构收敛保证之间的鸿沟，为数据驱动动态系统学习提供了兼具灵活性和理论保证的新框架。

局限 / 后续问题: 输入到状态收缩性质仅在有界外部激励条件下成立，对于无界输入的情形尚未讨论。

中文摘要: 从观测数据中学习动态系统的连续时间表示已成为数据驱动控制和科学机器学习的基石。然而，现有的神经微分方程要么以启发式方式处理外部控制输入而不提供严格的结构保证，要么在恒定或消失输入的受限假设下强制执行稳定性。本文提出了输入收缩神经微分模型（ICNDM），一种新型深度学习框架，能够无缝融合时变控制输入，同时通过输入依赖的收缩正则化确保增量指数收敛。通过利用嵌入式输入编码器和参数化度量网络，所提架构同时学习非自治神经向量场和广义黎曼收缩度量。我们推导了输入依赖收缩的充分条件，并在有界外部激励下形式化地建立了输入到状态收缩性质。在高度非线性混沌振荡器和永磁同步电机（PMSM）驱动系统实验数据上的大量数值评估表明，与最先进的神经微分基准相比，ICNDM在长期展开误差上实现了大幅降低，并展现出对输入扰动的优越结构鲁棒性。

英文原始摘要: Learning continuous-time representations of dynamical systems from observation data has emerged as a cornerstone of data-driven control and scientific machine learning. However, existing neural differential equations either treat external control inputs heuristically without providing strict structural guarantees, or enforce stability properties under the restrictive assumption of constant or vanishing inputs. This paper proposes the Input-Contraction Neural Differential Model (ICNDM), a novel deep learning framework that seamlessly incorporates time-varying control inputs while ensuring incremental exponential convergence via input-dependent contraction regularization. By leveraging an embedded input encoder and a parameterized metric network, the proposed architecture learns both the non-autonomous neural vector fields and a generalized Riemannian contraction metric simultaneously. We derive sufficient conditions for input-dependent contraction and formally establish an input-to-state contraction property under bounded external excitations. Extensive numerical evaluations on highly nonlinear chaotic oscillators and experimental data from a Permanent Magnet Synchronous Motor (PMSM) drive system demonstrate that ICNDM yields substantial reductions in long-horizon rollout errors and exhibits superior structural robustness against input perturbations compared with state-of-the-art neural differential benchmarks.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 超导二极管效应可实现超导电流整流，但现有方案对外加磁场的依赖限制了器件的实用性与集成度，亟需无磁场方案。

主要方法: 构建耦合动力学模型，将极化依赖的RCSJ（电阻-电容分路结）描述与Landau-Khalatnikov-Tani铁电动力学方程相结合，模拟电流扫描下的结行为。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论研究，通过数值模拟分析铁电约瑟夫森结在电流扫描下的临界电流与回滞电流行为，考察铁电参数、扫描协议及有限温度对非互易性的影响。

主要结果: 铁电极化翻转导致临界电流和回滞电流在正反方向上不对称；非互易性可由铁电参数和扫描协议高度调控，且在有限温度下依然稳定。

结论: 铁电约瑟夫森结是实现无外磁场非互易超导器件的有前景平台，为超导整流器件设计提供了新思路。

局限 / 后续问题: 摘要未明确提及实验验证，目前仅限于理论模型与数值模拟，尚需实验确认。

中文摘要: 超导二极管效应（SDE）表现为相反电流方向下临界超导电流不相等，能够实现超导电流整流。我们在具有破缺反演对称性的铁电约瑟夫森结中提出了一种无外磁场条件下的赝超导二极管效应。利用将极化依赖的RCSJ描述与Landau-Khalatnikov-Tani铁电动力学相结合的耦合动力学模型，我们展示了铁电极化翻转在电流扫描下会引起不对称的临界电流和回滞电流。由此产生的非互易性可通过铁电参数和扫描协议进行高度调控，并在有限温度下保持稳定。我们的工作表明，铁电约瑟夫森结是实现无外磁场非互易超导器件的有前景的平台。

英文原始摘要: The superconducting diode effect (SDE), characterized by unequal critical supercurrents in opposite current directions, enables supercurrent rectification. We propose a magnetic-field-free pseudo-superconducting-diode effect in ferroelectric Josephson junctions with broken inversion symmetry. Using a coupled dynamical model that combines a polarization-dependent RCSJ description with Landau-Khalatnikov-Tani ferroelectric dynamics, we show that ferroelectric polarization switching induces asymmetric critical and retrapping currents under current sweeps. The resulting nonreciprocity is highly tunable via ferroelectric parameters and the sweep protocol and remains robust at finite temperatures. Our work identifies ferroelectric Josephson junctions as a promising platform for magnetic-field-free nonreciprocal superconducting devices.

</details>

结论: 所提方法能够有效构建具有扩展辅助变量的马尔可夫模型，准确参数化记忆效应显著的多维粗粒化系统的运动方程，且避免了记忆核逆问题的求解。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们提出一种数值算法，用于构建具有扩展变量列表的马尔可夫模型，以参数化在记忆效应显著时处于外部势中的多维粗粒化物理系统的运动方程。该方法利用稳态速度的自相关数据，但在第一步中避免了从这些数据中寻找相应记忆核的逆问题。相反，数据被用于构建自相关函数的Prony级数近似，该Prony级数的参数提供了相应的马尔可夫模型。分子动力学数据的数值结果表明，参数化模型在一维系统中使用五个辅助变量、在二维系统中使用十二个辅助变量时具有良好的匹配效果。

英文原始摘要: We propose a numerical algorithm to construct a Markov model with an extended list of variables to parameterize the equation of motion of a multidimensional coarse-grained physical system in an external potential, when memory effects are relevant. Our method uses autocorrelation data of the stationary velocities, but it avoids the inverse problem of finding the corresponding memory kernel from these data in a first step. Rather, the data are used to construct a Prony series approximation of the autocorrelation function, and the parameters of this Prony series provide the corresponding Markov model. Numerical results for molecular dynamics data show a good match for parameterized models with five auxiliary variables for a one-dimensional, and twelve auxiliary variables for a two-dimensional system.

</details>

主要结果: 模型适定性得到证明；参数估计基于实际数据完成，支持水温影响香鱼洄游的假设；模型在环境DNA数据分析中的应用潜力得到初步探讨。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们对随机环境中河流固定位置观测到的洄游鱼类数量的动态变化进行了数学建模。特别地，作为一种新方法，我们构建了一个随机微分方程，将环境因素对洄游开始和结束波动的影响纳入其中。该模型是一个具有非Lipschitz扩散系数的扩散桥，称为Cox-Ingersoll-Ross桥（CIR桥），并通过时间变换产生随机的初始和终止时刻，从而能够有效地纳入环境因素的影响。我们首先证明了模型的适定性，这在应用数学中被认为是新颖且重要的。其次，我们基于香鱼（Plecoglossus altivelis altivelis）上游洄游的最新多年日数据集，在水温影响鱼类洄游这一已有研究提出的假设下，对模型参数进行了估计。我们还探讨了所提模型在环境DNA数据分析这一具有挑战性任务中的应用。本研究推动了既能考虑环境因素又简洁的鱼类洄游理论的发展。

英文原始摘要: We mathematically model the dynamics of the number of migratory fish observed at a fixed location along a river in a random environment. Particularly, as a new approach, we construct a stochastic differential equation that incorporates the influence of environmental factors on the fluctuations in the start and end of migration. The model is a diffusion bridge with a non-Lipschitz diffusion coefficient, called the Cox-Ingersoll-Ross bridge, and has random initial and terminal times arising from time-change, so that the influences of environmental factors can be efficiently incorporated. The well-posedness of the model is first established, which is considered novel and significant in applied mathematics. Second, we estimate the parameters of the model based on the latest multiyear daily data set for the upstream migration of Plecoglossus altivelis altivelis (Ayu) by relying on the hypothesis that water temperature affects the migration of the fish, which has been suggested in existing studies. We also explore the application of the proposed model to the challenging task of analyzing environmental DNA data. This study advances the development of a theory of fish migration that is simple yet can take environmental factors into account.

</details>

<details>

主要结果: 预警指标的有效性取决于系统性质，特别是Hurst指数和分岔类型；Hopf分岔的旋转动力学显著改变了这些可观测量的标度律。

结论: 为非马尔可夫系统中早期预警信号的实施提供了实用指南，并在理论和应用模型上得到验证。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们为非马尔可夫系统中即将发生的临界转换构建了早期预警信号。我们分析了表现出此类转换的快慢系统中的随机强迫，如分数布朗运动、分数Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程和红噪声。我们证明了自协方差、自相关和谱密度等指标的有效性取决于底层系统的若干性质。特别地，我们比较了Hurst指数和分岔类型的影响。我们证明了与Hopf分岔相关的旋转动力学显著改变了这些可观测量的标度律。最后，我们提供了实施这些信号的实用指南，并在理论和应用模型上进行了验证。

英文原始摘要: We construct early-warning signals for impending critical transitions in non-Markovian systems. We analyze stochastic forcings such as fractional Brownian motion, fractional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes and red noise in fast-slow systems exhibiting such transitions. We show that the effectiveness of indicators such as autocovariance, autocorrelation, and spectral density depends on several properties of the underlying system. In particular, we compare the influence of the Hurst index and the bifurcation type. We prove that the rotatory dynamics associated with a Hopf bifurcation substantially alters the scaling laws of these observables. Finally, we provide practical guidelines for implementing these signals and validate them on both theoretical and applied models.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 物理系统的动态响应需要遵循时间因果性和时间不变性，而现有的DeepONet架构缺乏对这些基本时间特性的自然尊重，限制了其在动态系统中的准确性与适用性。

主要方法: 通过结合TDNN和DeepONet构建TINO架构。将带初值的时间依赖偏微分方程视为截断的时间不变系统，并在输出函数空间中应用空间POD方法进行求解扩展。

实验 / 验证: 进行了多种数值测试，对比了TINO、具有时间因果性的神经算子以及不具备时间特性的神经算子的表现。

主要结果: 数值测试结果证明，所提出的TINO神经算子框架相比其他对比模型具有更高的预测精度。

结论: 通过结合时间延迟神经网络与DeepONet以尊重时间不变性等物理特性，能够有效提升算子学习在动态系统及时间依赖偏微分方程中的求解精度。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 深度算子网络（DeepONet）是使用神经网络学习非线性算子的基础架构之一。然而，对于描述物理系统动态响应的算子，DeepONet并不能自然地遵循基本的时间特性，包括时间因果性和时间不变性。为了克服这些局限性，我们提出了时间不变神经算子（TINO），通过建立时间延迟神经网络（TDNN）和DeepONet之间的联系来实现。随后，我们将该提议扩展到求解带有初值的时间依赖偏微分方程，这可以被视为截断的时间不变系统，并在输出函数空间中实现了空间本征正交分解（POD）。通过比较TINO、具有时间因果性的神经算子以及没有时间特性的神经算子的各种数值测试，证实了所提出的神经算子框架具有更高的精度。

英文原始摘要: The deep operator network (DeepONet) is one of the basic architectures for learning nonlinear operators with neural networks. However, for operators that describe the dynamic response of physical systems, DeepONet does not naturally respect fundamental time properties, including time causality and time invariance. We propose time-invariant neural operator (TINO) to overcome these limitations, by creating the connection between time-delay neural network (TDNN) and DeepONet. We then extend the proposal to solving time-dependent PDEs with initial values, which can be treated as truncated time-invariant systems, with spatial proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) implementation in the output function space. Various numerical tests comparing TINO, neural operators with time causality, and those without time properties justify the enhanced precision of the proposed neural operator frameworks.

</details>

研究问题: 传统基于价格的均场储能协调模型假设智能体响应真实的群体平均充电功率，使得通信拓扑无关紧要。当智能体改为响应共享的噪声预测时，通信拓扑是否会显现出可检测的动力学效应？

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 在分布式储能协调中，智能体通常只能获取平均量的噪声预测而非真实值，需要理解这种更现实的设定下通信拓扑对集群动力学的影响。

主要方法: 构建智能体响应共享噪声预测的均场模型，引入预测误差相关系数ρ，通过解析推导和数值仿真分析拓扑结构对集群有效维度响应的影响，并利用谱参与比等指标刻画共识模式与横向模式的相对贡献。

实验 / 验证: 在线形图、星形图和小世界图三种拓扑结构上进行仿真，比较拓扑引起的响应变异与重新抽取预测噪声所导致的变异。

主要结果: 拓扑结构在集群有效维度响应中不可检测；拓扑引起的变异低于重新抽取预测噪声所导致的变异；随着ρN增大，共识模式方差占主导，谱参与比趋近于1且与拓扑无关。

结论: 共享的随机强迫能够掩盖分布式储能集群中依赖于拓扑的动态模式，但这并不意味着拓扑结构没有动力学效应。

局限 / 后续问题: 该结论仅限于所研究的共享噪声预测体制，未涵盖拓扑效应可能显现的其他场景；结果不排除拓扑在其他可观测量或参数体制下产生可检测影响的可能性。

中文摘要: 基于价格的电池储能协调均场模型通常假设每个智能体对真实的群体平均充电功率做出响应。在该假设下，通信拓扑结构无关紧要，因为广播价格已经承载了关键的耦合信息。我们研究了一个相近的体制，其中智能体对平均值的共享噪声预测做出响应，各智能体预测误差之间的相关系数为ρ。通过解析分析和仿真，我们发现即使邻居观测是唯一的显式通信信号，拓扑结构在储能集群的有效维度响应中仍然不可检测。其机制是结构性的：相关的预测误差投影到图不变的共识模式上，而拓扑结构则通过横向模式起作用。随着ρN增大，共识模式方差占主导地位，谱参与比趋向于1，且与图拓扑无关。在线形图、星形图和小世界图上的仿真证实，拓扑引起的变异低于重新抽取预测噪声所导致的变异。该结果并非声称拓扑结构没有动力学效应，而是表明共享的随机强迫可以掩盖分布式储能集群中依赖于拓扑的模式。

英文原始摘要: Price-based mean-field models of battery storage coordination usually assume that each agent responds to the true population-average charging power. Under that assumption, communication topology is irrelevant because the broadcast price already carries the coupling that matters. We study a nearby regime in which agents respond to a shared noisy forecast of the average, with correlation rho between agents' forecast errors. Analytically and in simulation, we find that topology remains undetectable in the effective-dimensional response of the fleet, even when neighbour observation is the only explicit communication signal. The mechanism is structural: the correlated forecast error projects onto the graph-invariant consensus mode, while topology acts through transverse modes. As rho N grows, the consensus-mode variance dominates and the spectral participation ratio approaches one independently of graph topology. Simulations on linear, star, and small-world graphs confirm that topology-induced variation is below the variation caused by redrawing the forecast noise. The result is not a claim that topology has no dynamical effect, but that shared stochastic forcing can mask topology-dependent modes in decentralized storage fleets.

</details>

*Ana Fernandez Navamuel,A. Javier Omella,Diego Zamora-Sanchez,David Pardo*

一句话结论: 提出物理信息高斯Copula变分自编码器，用于桥梁结构健康监测中的不确定性量化损伤识别。

研究问题: 基于振动的结构损伤识别是病态逆问题，现有确定性深度学习方法缺乏不确定性量化且可能产生物理不一致的结果，难以应对测量噪声、稀疏传感和环境变异等挑战。

主要创新点: 将可微分数值特征值求解器直接嵌入VAE架构以保证物理一致性，并用高斯Copula替代潜在变量独立性假设以捕获相邻结构单元间的空间交叉相关性。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 确定性深度学习方法缺乏可靠的不确定性量化，且在噪声、稀疏传感器和环境变异性条件下易产生物理上不一致的损伤识别结果，需要一种兼顾物理约束与不确定性量化的概率框架。

主要方法: 提出PI-GCVAE框架：将可微分数值特征值求解器嵌入VAE确保潜在空间样本满足结构动力学方程；引入高斯Copula建模潜在变量的复杂空间交叉相关性，替代传统独立性假设；相较于高斯混合模型，在高维强相关潜在空间中提供更高效的分布建模。

实验 / 验证: 在简支梁桥的合成数据集上验证，包含多种损伤场景并叠加随机高斯噪声；利用合成数据可获取真实刚度值作为基准进行详尽验证。

主要结果: PI-GCVAE准确恢复了真实后验分布，达到77.2%的覆盖率，证明其在损伤识别中的可靠性和有效性。

结论: PI-GCVAE为运营桥梁的早期损伤诊断提供了一种可靠、可扩展的工具，能够同时满足物理一致性和不确定性量化需求。

局限 / 后续问题: 仅通过合成数据验证，未涉及真实桥梁实测数据；高斯Copula假设可能无法捕获所有类型的非线性依赖关系（如尾部依赖）。

中文摘要: 基于振动的土木基础设施损伤识别是一个具有挑战性的病态逆问题，其困难源于测量噪声、稀疏传感器阵列和环境变异性。尽管深度学习在系统辨识方面功能强大，但确定性方法缺乏可靠的不确定性量化，且可能产生物理上不一致的结果。本文提出了一种稳健的概率科学机器学习（SciML）框架：用于结构健康监测（SHM）的物理信息高斯Copula变分自编码器（PI-GCVAE）。首先，通过将可微分数值特征值求解器直接嵌入VAE架构中，消除了对数据驱动代理模型的需求。这确保了潜在空间样本满足结构动力学的控制方程，减少了可训练参数空间并提高了泛化能力。其次，用高斯Copula取代了潜在变量的传统独立性假设。该模型捕获了相邻结构单元之间复杂的、依赖物理的空间交叉相关性，在考虑固有系统变异性和测量误差的同时定义了可行解。第三，与高斯混合等替代方案相比，基于Copula的VAE为高维、强相关的潜在空间提供了高效的分布模型。我们使用简支梁桥在多种损伤场景下并受随机高斯噪声污染的合成数据集对该方法进行了验证。合成数据使得能够针对实践中无法获取的真实刚度值进行详尽验证。结果表明，PI-GCVAE准确恢复了真实后验分布，实现了77.2%的覆盖率。所提出的框架为运营桥梁的早期损伤诊断提供了可靠、可扩展的工具。

英文原始摘要: Vibration-based damage identification in civil infrastructure is a challenging, ill-posed inverse problem due to measurement noise, sparse sensor arrays, and environmental variability. While deep learning is powerful for system identification, deterministic approaches lack reliable uncertainty quantification and can yield physically inconsistent results. This work proposes a robust probabilistic Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) framework: a physics-informed Gaussian copula variational autoencoder (PI-GCVAE) for structural health monitoring (SHM). First, we eliminate the need for data-driven surrogates by embedding a differentiable numerical eigenvalue solver directly into the VAE architecture. This ensures that latent space samples satisfy the governing equations of structural dynamics, reducing the trainable parameter space and improving generalization. Second, we replace the conventional independence assumption of latent variables with a Gaussian copula. This model captures complex, physics-dependent spatial cross-correlations between adjacent structural elements, defining feasible solutions while accounting for inherent system variability and measurement errors. Third, compared with alternatives such as Gaussian mixtures, our copula-based VAE provides an efficient distributional model for high-dimensional, strongly correlated latent spaces. We validate the approach using a synthetic dataset of a simply supported bridge subjected to various damage scenarios and corrupted with stochastic Gaussian noise. Synthetic data enables exhaustive validation against ground-truth stiffness values unavailable in practice. Results demonstrate that the PI-GCVAE accurately recovers the true posterior distribution, achieving 77.2% coverage. The proposed framework provides a reliable, scalable tool for early-stage damage diagnosis in operating bridges.

</details>

*Pere Diaz-Lozano,Giulia Di Nunno*

研究问题: 倒向随机Volterra积分方程（BSVIE）的鞅被积函数在一般条件下缺乏Hölder型正则性估计，且其离散数值逼近缺乏收敛速率的理论保证。

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 为了在一般性假设下建立BSVIE鞅被积函数的正则性理论，并利用该理论为数值离散方法提供收敛速率保证。

主要方法: 使用BSDE系统逼近BSVIE；对BSDE系统应用Malliavin变分建立鞅分量的均匀正则性估计；通过取极限将结果转移至BSVIE；提出显式Euler格式进行离散逼近。

主要结果: 在 $L^p(Ω)$ 中获得了关于第二个时间变量 $1/2$ 阶的平均Hölder正则性估计，并证明该估计为所提出的显式Euler离散格式提供了明确的收敛速率。

结论: 在一般Malliavin可微条件下成功建立了BSVIE鞅被积函数的Hölder型正则性，并基于此给出了离散逼近算法的收敛速率。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们证明了倒向随机Volterra积分方程（BSVIE）的鞅被积函数的Hölder型正则性估计。该估计在第一个时间变量上进行 $L^2$ 平均后在 $L^p(Ω)$ 中表述，并在第二个时间变量上给出了 $1/2$ 阶的平均Hölder估计。我们的方法基于用BSDE系统对BSVIE进行逼近。对于该系统，我们利用Malliavin变分建立了鞅分量的均匀正则性估计，然后取极限得到了BSVIE的结果。我们允许一般的Malliavin可微自由项和生成元。特别地，不假设它们来自正向随机微分方程或具有特定的函数形式。我们还为逼近的BSDE系统提出了一种显式Euler格式，并证明了正则性估计为BSVIE的离散逼近提供了收敛速率。

英文原始摘要: We prove a Hölder-type regularity estimate for the martingale integrand of a backward stochastic Volterra integral equation (BSVIE). The estimate is formulated in $L^p(Ω)$ after averaging in $L^2$ over the first time variable, and gives an averaged Hölder estimate of order $1/2$ in the second time variable. Our approach is based on the approximation of the BSVIE by a system of BSDEs. For this system, we establish a uniform regularity estimate for the martingale component using Malliavin calculus, and then pass to the limit to obtain the result for the BSVIE. We allow for a general Malliavin differentiable free term and generator. In particular, neither is assumed to come from a forward stochastic differential equation or to have a specific functional form. We also propose an explicit Euler scheme for the approximating BSDE system and show that the regularity estimate yields a convergence rate for the resulting discrete approximation of the BSVIE.

</details>

*Mohammad Abedi*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06355v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.06355v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Entropic+Dynamics+of+Jump-Diffusion+Option+Pricing++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 从信息约束推导股价动力学，统一得出Merton跳跃扩散与期权定价框架

研究问题: 标准股价动力学与期权定价模型直接假设随机过程，缺乏从信息约束出发的统一推导框架，且在不完备市场中鞅测度的选择缺乏信息论依据。

主要创新点: 提出基于熵推断的框架，利用市场奖励收益率而非价格水平这一对称性选择对数价格为动力学变量，将价格变化分解为可因子分解的连续通道与跳跃通道，从信息约束自然推导出Merton跳跃扩散及Esscher变换。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 传统模型直接假设随机过程而非从基本原理推导，不同模型间缺乏统一的推断基础。市场奖励收益率而非价格水平这一对称性提示可用信息约束自然推导动力学过程。

主要方法: 采用熵推断框架，将对数价格作为动力学变量，设置连续通道（连续性与方向性约束）和跳跃通道（到达率及跳跃规模前两阶矩），利用约束作用于微观状态不相交部分实现通道因子分解，推导动力学方程；通过均值对数回报施加无套利条件选择鞅测度。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论推导，未涉及数值或实证实验，主要通过对动力学方程、期权定价方程及极限情形的理论分析验证框架的一致性。

主要结果: 推导出Merton跳跃扩散动力学，几何布朗运动为其无跳跃极限；对数价格密度满足Kolmogorov-Feller方程；无套利条件自然选择Esscher变换；期权价格满足Merton偏积分微分方程；风险中性对数正态混合分布生成隐含波动率微笑；跳跃消失时恢复Black-Scholes结果。

结论: 不同模型间的差异不在于推断方法本身，而在于输入推断过程的信息内容；熵推断提供了一个统一框架，可从信息约束自然推导多种经典股价动力学与期权定价模型。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 股价动力学与期权估值的标准模型通常从假设随机过程出发。本文发展了一个熵推断框架，从信息约束中推导这些过程。关键的对称性在于市场奖励的是收益率而非价格水平，这选择了对数价格作为动力学变量。价格变化由两个通道表示。连续通道承载连续性与方向性约束。跳跃通道承载到达率与跳跃规模的前两阶矩。由于这些约束适用于微观状态的不相交部分，两个通道可因子分解。所得动力学为Merton跳跃扩散，几何布朗运动为其无跳跃极限。对数价格密度满足Kolmogorov-Feller方程，其无跳跃极限为Fokker-Planck方程。同一推断原理在通过均值对数回报施加无套利条件后，从不完备市场中的众多鞅测度中选择了Esscher变换。期权价格随后满足Merton偏积分微分方程，风险中性对数正态分布混合生成隐含波动率微笑。当跳跃消失时恢复Black-Scholes结果。模型之间的差异不在于推断本身，而在于输入其中的信息。

英文原始摘要: Standard models of stock price dynamics and option valuation usually begin by postulating stochastic processes. This paper develops an entropic inference framework that derives these processes from information constraints. The key symmetry is that markets reward returns rather than price levels, which selects log price as the dynamical variable. Price changes are represented by two channels. The continuous channel carries constraints of continuity and directionality. The jump channel carries the arrival rate and the first two moments of jump size. Since these constraints apply to disjoint parts of the microstate, the channels factorize. The resulting dynamics is the Merton jump diffusion, with Geometric Brownian Motion as the no jump limit. The log price density satisfies the Kolmogorov Feller equation, whose no jump limit is the Fokker Planck equation. The same inferential principle, with no arbitrage imposed through the mean log return, selects the Esscher transform from the many martingale measures available in an incomplete market. The option price then satisfies Mertons partial integro differential equation, and the risk neutral mixture of lognormal distributions generates the implied volatility smile. The Black Scholes results are recovered when jumps vanish. What changes from one model to another is not the inference, but the information supplied to it.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 现有DSA方法依赖正交对齐，但正交对齐无法正确刻画拓扑共轭关系：共轭系统可能需要非正交基转移矩阵，非共轭系统可能具有正交等价的Koopman算子，因此需要更严格的对齐方法。

主要方法: 基于正交对齐不足性的理论分析，构造CSA方法，将对齐约束为候选状态空间双射诱导的对齐；证明CSA拟合对齐与复合算子的有限数据投影关系；通过受控示例验证。

结论: 研究结果澄清了基于Koopman的相似性度量必须满足的条件，才能支持识别计算系统之间共轭性的主张，为后续方法设计提供了理论指导。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 比较两个动力系统是否在坐标或测量差异下实现相同的计算，是神经科学和机器学习中的核心问题。动态相似性分析[DSA; Ostrow等, 2023]通过正交相似变换对齐有限维Koopman近似来解决该问题。本文证明正交对齐对于拓扑共轭既非必要也非充分：共轭系统可能需要DSA无法捕获的非正交基转移矩阵，而非共轭系统可能具有正交等价的Koopman算子，使DSA无法区分。基于此观察，我们提出共轭相似性分析(CSA)，将对齐限制为由候选状态空间双射诱导的对齐，而非任意正交矩阵。我们证明CSA的拟合对齐是与候选双射相关联的复合算子的有限数据投影，并通过受控示例说明当可观测字典从数据中显式或隐式选取时，这一区别为何重要。这些结果澄清了基于Koopman的相似性度量必须确保什么，才能支持识别计算系统之间共轭性的主张。

英文原始摘要: Comparing whether two dynamical systems implement the same computation despite differences in coordinates or measurements is a central problem in neuroscience and machine learning. Dynamical Similarity Analysis [DSA; Ostrow et al., 2023] addresses this problem by aligning finite-dimensional Koopman approximations through an orthogonal similarity transformation. Here we show that orthogonal alignment is neither necessary nor sufficient for topological conjugacy: conjugate systems may require a non-orthogonal basis-transfer matrix that DSA cannot capture, while non-conjugate systems may have orthogonally equivalent Koopman operators that DSA fails to distinguish. We use this observation to formulate Conjugacy-based Similarity Analysis (CSA), which restricts alignments to those induced by candidate state-space bijections rather than arbitrary orthogonal matrices. We prove that CSA's fitted alignment is the finite-data projection of the composition operator associated with the candidate bijection, and use controlled examples to show why this distinction matters when observable dictionaries are chosen explicitly or implicitly from data. These results clarify what Koopman-based similarity measures must ensure to support claims of identifying conjugacies between computational systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 视频是互联网上最具吸引力的内容模态，理解观众反应如何随视频中随时间展开的处理特征而变化是一个核心因果问题，但现有方法无法应对视频场景中的高维潜在混淆挑战。

主要方法: 分三步：(1) 用深度生成模型重建视频并提取内部表示作为低维摘要；(2) 证明动态随机干预下平均潜在结果轨迹的非参数可识别性；(3) 提出基于纵向神经网络架构的一致且渐近正态的估计量。

实验 / 验证: 构建包含10,000个超级马里奥兄弟关卡的新因果推断基准（由固定AI智能体游玩，真实因果效应已知），并将方法应用于2020年美国总统竞选电视广告数据。

主要结果: 在超级马里奥基准上实证验证了方法的有效性；在电视广告应用中发现随时间增加候选人出现概率可带来更高的平均观众评价。

结论: 所提方法使研究者能够探究视频中哪些视觉特征在哪些时间点影响观众反应，并可在具有已知真实因果效应的数据集上对方法进行基准测试。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们开发了首个以视频特征作为处理的因果推断统计方法。视频是互联网上最具吸引力的内容模态。一个核心的因果问题是：观众反应如何随视频中随时间展开的处理特征而变化。然而，标准因果推断方法无法适用，因为混淆特征是潜在的、高维的，且与处理序列和结果轨迹动态相关。为应对这些挑战，我们首先利用深度生成模型重建每个视频，并利用模型的内部表示作为视频内容的学习型低维摘要用于因果估计。然后，我们证明了在动态随机干预下，平均潜在结果轨迹是非参数可识别的。最后，我们提出了一种基于纵向神经网络架构的一致且渐近正态的估计量。我们通过构建一个新的因果推断基准来实证验证本方法，该基准包含由固定的马里奥AI智能体游玩的10,000个超级马里奥兄弟关卡，其中真实因果效应由构造已知。最后，我们将该方法应用于2020年美国总统竞选的电视广告，发现随时间增加候选人出现的概率会带来更高的平均观众评价。借助所提出的方法，研究人员可以探究视频中哪些视觉特征在哪些时间点影响观众反应，同时可在具有已知真实因果效应的数据集上对方法进行基准测试。

英文原始摘要: We develop the first statistical methodology for causal inference with video features as treatments. Video is the most engaging content modality on the internet. A central causal question is how audience reactions change in response to treatment features that unfold over the course of a video. Unfortunately, standard causal inference methods are not applicable because confounding features are latent, high-dimensional, and dynamically related to both the treatment sequence and the outcome trajectory. To address these challenges, we first reproduce each video using a deep generative model and leverage the model's internal representations as learned, low-dimensional summaries of video content for causal estimation. We then establish that the average potential-outcome trajectory under dynamic stochastic interventions is nonparametrically identified. Lastly, we propose a consistent and asymptotically normal estimator based on a longitudinal neural network architecture. We empirically validate our approach by constructing a new causal inference benchmark consisting of $10{,}000$ Super Mario Bros. levels played by fixed Mario AI agents, where ground-truth causal effects are known by construction. Finally, we apply our method to television advertisements from the 2020 U.S. presidential campaign and find that increasing the probability of a candidate appearing over time leads to higher average viewer evaluations. With the proposed methodology, researchers can ask which visual features, appearing at which points in a video, influence audience responses, while benchmarking new methods against datasets with known ground-truth causal effects.

</details>

研究问题: 如何从运动物体的时间序列数据中准确估计Cummins方程中的流体动力学系数（附加质量、阻尼系数、静水恢复力等）缺乏有效的方法。

主要创新点: 将Cummins方程表述为一阶系统并嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINN），同时完成结构状态估计与流体动力参数的逆问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 浮式海上结构物等运动体的流体动力学系数对结构设计与分析至关重要，传统估计方法存在局限性，需要利用运动数据进行高效准确的数据驱动估计。

主要方法: 将Cummins方程作为一阶系统嵌入PINN，结合可用的运动数据，先估计结构的平移和旋转自由度状态，再求解逆问题确定附加质量、阻尼系数和/或静水恢复力等流体动力参数。

结论: 所提基于PINN的方法可有效利用运动数据估计Cummins方程中的状态和流体动力学系数，为浮式海上结构物的流体动力分析提供了新的数据驱动途径。

局限 / 后续问题: 估计精度依赖于运动数据的数量和质量，数据不足或质量较差时精度可能受限。

中文摘要: 我们提出了一种利用运动物体（如浮式海上结构物）的时间序列数据来估计Cummins方程中流体动力学系数的方法。该数据驱动方法将控制结构与水波相互作用的Cummins方程结合到物理信息神经网络（PINN）中，并利用可用的运动数据。该方法首先估计结构在平移和旋转自由度方向上的状态，然后求解逆问题以确定作用于物体上的流体动力，以附加质量、阻尼系数和/或静水恢复力表示。Cummins方程被表述为一阶系统，状态估计和参数估计均使用PINN完成。该方法在球体和箱体的自由衰减运动上进行了验证。结果表明，可以准确估计状态和流体动力学系数，但精度取决于运动数据的数量和质量。

英文原始摘要: We present a method for estimating the hydrodynamic coefficients in the Cummins equations using time-series data from a moving body, such as a floating offshore structure. The proposed data-driven method is based on incorporating the Cummins equations governing the dynamics of a structural body interacting with water waves into a physics-informed neural network (PINN), along with available motion data. The proposed method first estimates the structure's state in terms of translational and rotational degrees of freedom, and then solves the inverse problem to determine the hydrodynamic forces acting on the body, expressed in terms of added mass, damping coefficients, and/or hydrostatic restoring. The Cummins equations are formulated as a first-order system, and both state and parameter estimation are performed using PINNs. The method is verified on the free decay of a sphere and a box. The results demonstrate that it is possible to estimate the state and hydrodynamic coefficients accurately, although accuracy depends on the volume and quality of the movement data.

</details>

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 生成式建模的最新进展通过利用受流匹配启发的部分无模拟训练方法，使得在高维环境下高效计算薛定谔桥（SB）成为可能。然而，这些方法尚未涵盖具有反射动力学的薛定谔桥，这是一种有用的模型选择，具有生成样本保持在数据域内的内置保证。现有的反射薛定谔桥替代方案则依赖于基于前向-后向SDE理论的更复杂训练方法，需要昂贵的高阶导数并在训练期间采样整条路径。在本文中，我们引入了一个部分无模拟框架，使得反射薛定谔桥能够类似于流匹配进行训练，并使用了一种新的采样方法和回归目标。我们通过耦合知名的高维图像数据集对来展示实验结果。使用反射动力学在训练和推理期间仅产生可忽略的额外墙钟时间，同时维持或略微提升生成性能。

英文原始摘要: Recent advances in generative modeling have enabled the efficient computation of Schrödinger bridges (SB) in high-dimensional settings by leveraging partially simulation-free training methods inspired by flow matching. However, these have not covered SBs with reflecting dynamics, a useful model choice with built-in guarantees that generated samples stay in the data domain. Existing alternatives for reflected SBs instead rely on more complex training based on forward--backward SDE theory, requiring expensive higher-order derivatives and sampling entire paths during training. In this article, we introduce a partially simulation-free framework that allows reflected SBs to be trained similarly to flow matching, using a new sampling method and regression target. We demonstrate our results by coupling pairs of well-known high-dimensional image datasets. Using reflected dynamics incurs negligible additional wall-clock time during both training and inference while maintaining or slightly improving generative performance.

</details>

主要创新点: 提出了一种基于截断路径签名和梯度提升分类器的非参数化模型识别框架，通过提取样本路径的几何特征进行模型分类。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 旨在摆脱对参数校准的依赖，利用波动率样本路径的几何特征实现对不同随机波动率模型类别的有效区分与识别。

主要方法: 将波动率轨迹通过截断路径签名映射到特征空间，然后应用梯度提升分类器来区分不同的波动率动态模型类别。

实验 / 验证: 在多种设置下进行数值实验，包括结构不同的模型、具有相近赫斯特参数的粗糙波动率模型，以及在随机抽样参数下的不确定性测试，并详细分析了Heston与Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型之间的误分类模式。

主要结果: 该方法在各种设置下均实现了高分类准确率，并在参数不确定性下依然有效。大部分判别信息由签名的前四层捕获，高阶项的改进微乎其微。

结论: 随机波动率模型可以通过其样本路径的几何特征得到有效识别，支持了基于签名的非参数方法在模型识别任务中的可行性。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们提出了一种基于签名特征的框架，用于从观测到的路径数据中识别随机波动率模型类别。通过截断路径签名将波动率轨迹映射到特征空间，并应用梯度提升分类器，我们证明了可以在不依赖参数校准的情况下区分不同类别的波动率动态。通过一系列数值实验，我们证明了该方法在多种设置下都能实现高分类准确率，从结构不同的模型到具有紧密间隔赫斯特参数的粗糙波动率模型。我们表明该方法在参数不确定性下依然有效，其中每条模拟路径都是使用随机采样的模型参数生成的，并且详细分析了Heston模型和Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型之间在波动率参数方面的误分类模式。结果强调，大部分相关的判别信息被签名的前四个层级捕获，而高阶项仅提供微小的改进。总体而言，研究结果支持了这样一个观点：随机波动率模型可以通过其样本路径的几何特征进行有效识别。

英文原始摘要: We propose a signature-based framework for the identification of stochastic volatility model classes from observed path data. By mapping volatility trajectories into a feature space via truncated path signatures and applying a gradient boosting classifier, we show that it is possible to distinguish between different classes of volatility dynamics without relying on parametric calibration.
  Through a series of numerical experiments, we demonstrate that the method achieves high classification accuracy across a range of settings, from structurally distinct models to cases involving rough volatility models with closely spaced Hurst parameters. We show that the method remains effective under parameter uncertainty, where each simulated path is drawn with randomly sampled model parameters, and provide a detailed analysis of the misclassification pattern between the Heston and Ornstein--Uhlenbeck models in terms of the volatility of volatility parameter. The results highlight that most of the relevant discriminative information is captured by the first four levels of the signature, while higher-order terms provide only marginal improvements. Overall, the findings support the view that stochastic volatility models can be effectively identified through the geometry of their sample paths.

</details>

The operator describes nonlinear dynamics in a linear way through the lens of real- or complex-valued observable functions. Recently proposed data-driven techniques, like extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD), its kernelized variant, and machine-learning methods, can be used to generate finite-dimensional approximations accompanied by finite-data error bounds. In this tutorial paper, we provide a concise introduction into Koopman operator theory and its use in systems and control. A particular focus is put on data-driven surrogate models, their extension to systems with inputs, and controller design using Koopman operator theory. Moreover, we demonstrate the key techniques, i.e., EDMD and Koopman MPC. To this end, we provide simulation studies including source code on GitHub to enable the interested reader to experience the Koopman operator in systems and control step by step.

中文摘要: 许多现实世界系统被组织为网络，其中时空动力学沿着连接展开，而非在节点之间离散地展开。例子包括供水系统或燃气网络等公用事业网络、电网以及交通流网络。这类系统自然地被建模为度量图，其中边对应于在顶点处连接的一维欧几里得子空间。度量图独立于底层全局欧几里得空间，限制了典型PINNs和算子学习方法的直接应用。特别是像平流这样的输运动力学，需要一种能够捕捉图上反对称和长程依赖关系的方法，这本身就是一个挑战。我们提出了一种新颖的物理信息消息传递算子，将度量图上的线性平流编码为归纳偏置。在纯平流设置下，该算子可证明地在无需任何训练的情况下恢复精确动力学，仅存在理论推导的离散化误差。结合MLP等可训练组件，我们的消息传递算子扩展到供水系统中的现实平流-反应动力学，在此我们相比基线方法取得了优越的性能，并在不同图拓扑间实现了零样本泛化。

英文原始摘要: Many real-world systems are organized as networks where spatio-temporal dynamics unfold along connections and not discretely between nodes. Examples include utility networks such as water distribution systems or gas networks, electrical grids, and traffic flow networks. Such systems are naturally modeled as metric graphs, where edges correspond to one-dimensional Euclidean subspaces connected at vertices. Metric graphs are independent of an underlying global Euclidean space, limiting direct application of typical PINNs and operator-learning methods. Especially transport dynamics like advection require a methodology able to capture antisymmetric and long-range dependencies on graphs, which is itself a challenge. We propose a novel physics-informed message passing operator that encodes linear advection on metric graphs as an inductive bias. In the purely advective setting, the operator provably recovers the exact dynamics up to a theoretically derived discretization error without any training. Combined with trainable components like MLPs, our message passing operator extends to realistic advection-reaction dynamics in water distribution systems, where we achieve superior performance compared to baselines and zero-shot generalization across different graph topologies.

</details>

*Eszra Forenita Sigalingging,Liu Kin Men,Setianto Setianto,Ferry Faizal*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

一句话结论: 软质量守恒约束使PINN在长期模拟中大幅降低误差与质量漂移。

研究问题: PINNs仅强制PDE局部残差，无法保证长时间积分中质量等全局量的守恒，这对平流-扩散方程等守恒输运问题的长期模拟可靠性构成根本性限制。

主要创新点: 提出Mass-Penalty PINN，在标准PINN损失函数中引入软质量守恒约束，以缓解长时间积分中的质量漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 质量守恒是平流-扩散方程等输运现象的基本物理约束，而PINNs不保证全局守恒量在长时间积分中得以保持，因此需要研究该限制的程度并提出改进方案。

主要方法: 在标准PINN损失中增加质量守恒的软惩罚项构建Mass-Penalty PINN，将其与Vanilla PINN及Crank-Nicolson格式在周期性一维平流-扩散方程上进行系统性比较。

实验 / 验证: 在涵盖扩散主导到平流主导的一系列Peclet数下，分别在短期和长期两个模拟时间范围内，比较Vanilla PINN、Mass-Penalty PINN和Crank-Nicolson格式的精度与质量守恒性能。

主要结果: 短期模拟中Mass-Penalty PINN提升不一致；长期模拟中相对L2误差和质量守恒误差分别降低约9-67倍和15-215倍。Vanilla PINN的精度退化主要由质量漂移随时间累积所致。

结论: 引入软质量守恒约束显著提升PINN在守恒输运问题中的长期可靠性，有效缓解长时间模拟中的质量漂移问题。

局限 / 后续问题: 短期模拟中改进不一致；仅测试了一维周期性问题，向多维或非周期情形的推广尚待验证。

中文摘要: 平流-扩散方程是输运现象的基本模型，其中质量守恒是重要的物理约束。虽然Crank-Nicolson等经典格式在构造上保持这一性质，但物理信息神经网络（PINNs）仅强制执行控制偏微分方程的局部残差，因此无法保证在长积分时间范围内守恒质量等全局量。在本工作中，我们研究了这一局限性对周期性一维平流-扩散方程的影响程度，并评估了一种质量惩罚PINN，该方法在标准PINN损失函数中增加了软质量守恒约束。我们在涵盖扩散主导到平流主导的一系列Peclet数下，以及在代表短期和长期动力学的两个模拟时间范围内，比较了Vanilla PINN、质量惩罚PINN和Crank-Nicolson格式的性能。结果表明，对于短期模拟，质量惩罚PINN并不总是提供一致的精度提升。然而，对于长期模拟，在所测试的Peclet数范围内，质量惩罚PINN将相对L2误差和质量守恒误差分别降低约9-67倍和15-215倍。进一步分析表明，Vanilla PINN中观察到的精度退化主要由质量漂移随时间的累积引起。这些结果表明，引入软质量守恒约束可显著提高PINN在守恒输运问题中的长期可靠性，特别是在减缓长模拟时间范围内的质量漂移方面。

英文原始摘要: The advection-diffusion equation is a fundamental model of transport phenomena in which mass conservation is an essential physical constraint. While classical schemes such as Crank-Nicolson preserve this property by construction, Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) enforce only the local residual of the governing PDE and are therefore not guaranteed to conserve global quantities such as mass over long integration horizons. In this work, we examine the extent of this limitation for the periodic one-dimensional advection-diffusion equation and evaluate a Mass-Penalty PINN that augments the standard PINN loss with a soft mass-conservation constraint. We compare the performance of Vanilla PINN, Mass-Penalty PINN, and the Crank-Nicolson scheme across a range of Peclet numbers spanning diffusion-dominated to advection-dominated regimes, and over two simulation horizons representing short-term and long-term dynamics. The results show that, for short-term simulations, the Mass-Penalty PINN does not always provide a consistent improvement in accuracy. However, for long-term simulations, the Mass-Penalty PINN reduces the relative L2 error and mass conservation error by factors of approximately 9-67 and 15-215, respectively, compared with the Vanilla PINN, across the tested Peclet numbers. Further analysis reveals that the accuracy degradation observed in Vanilla PINN is predominantly caused by the accumulation of mass drift over time. These results demonstrate that incorporating a soft mass-conservation constraint substantially improves the long-term reliability of PINN for conservative transport problems, particularly in mitigating mass drift over extended simulation horizons.

</details>

结论: 本文成功在统一框架下同时建立了非平衡随机环境中二阶SDE的Smoluchowski-Kramers近似与Khasminskii平均原理，为多尺度随机系统的约化提供了理论基础。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 本文针对非平衡随机环境中的一类二阶随机微分方程（SDE），同时建立了Smoluchowski-Kramers近似和Khasminskii平均原理。该系统描述了质量为$m>0$的粒子在外力、摩擦和噪声作用下的运动，这些因素均依赖于涨落环境（如随机热浴）。环境由一个快变一阶SDE建模，其中参数$0<\varepsilon\ll 1$编码了时间尺度分离。在标度$m=\varepsilon^2$下，慢过程依概率收敛到具有平均漂移项和噪声诱导系数的有效动力学。我们的分析利用了逐路径分部积分公式以及与快动力学相关联的Poisson方程。最后，提供了数值实验进行验证。

英文原始摘要: This work establishes a simultaneous Smoluchowski-Kramers approximation and Khasminskii averaging principle for a class of second-order stochastic differential equations (SDEs) in nonequilibrium random environments. The system describes the motion of a particle of mass $m>0$ subject to external forces, friction, and noise, all of which depend on a fluctuating environment such as a stochastic heat bath. The environment is modeled by a fast-varying first-order SDE, where a parameter $0<ε\ll 1$ encodes the time-scale separation. Under the scaling $m=ε^2$, the slow process converges in probability to an effective dynamics with averaged drift and noise-induced coefficients. Our analysis utilizes a pathwise integration-by-parts formula and Poisson equations associated with the fast dynamics. Finally, numerical experiments are provided for demonstration.

</details>

主要方法: 利用第三次量化框架，将Fermi-Hubbard哈密顿量映射到作用于Majorana费米子的Lindblad跳跃算符；对动力学进行轨迹拆解，利用高斯性仅用两点关联函数模拟单条轨迹；分析有限粒子数所需的负耗散率及其导致的非马尔可夫动力学。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论分析，未涉及具体数值实验，但分析了轨迹拆解中正负随机权重导致的符号问题和涨落对收敛的影响。

主要结果: 单条轨迹可仅用两点关联函数多项式代价模拟；但有限粒子数要求负耗散率，引入非马尔可夫动力学，轨迹拆解出现符号问题和大的涨落，收敛需指数级多轨迹，总计算代价仍为指数级。

结论: 该映射在单轨迹层面具有高效的高斯表示，但非马尔可夫拆解的符号问题使总计算代价保持指数级；总代价关键依赖于非马尔可夫拆解的计算复杂度，有必要进一步研究此类拆解的效率。

局限 / 后续问题: 非马尔可夫动力学的轨迹拆解存在符号问题和大的涨落，导致收敛需要指数级多轨迹，总体计算代价仍为指数级；非马尔可夫拆解的效率尚未解决，需要进一步研究。

中文摘要: 我们引入了一种将相互作用费米子哈密顿量的基态与纯耗散开放量子系统的非平衡稳态之间的映射。在第三次量子化框架下，我们将Fermi-Hubbard哈密顿量映射到作用于Majorana费米子的Lindblad跳跃算符上。值得注意的是，跃迁项和相互作用项都映射到能够沿各量子轨迹保持Majorana费米子高斯性的跳跃算符。因此，动力学可以被拆解，每条轨迹可以仅使用两点关联函数进行高效模拟，其计算代价随系统大小多项式增长。我们进一步表明，有限粒子数需要负耗散率，从而导致内禀的非马尔可夫动力学。相应的轨迹拆解涉及正负随机权重，并表现出符号问题和大的涨落，因此收敛需要指数级大量的轨迹。尽管单条轨迹具有高效的高斯表示，总计算代价仍随系统大小指数增长，但关键取决于非马尔可夫拆解的计算复杂度，这激发了对此类拆解效率的进一步研究。

英文原始摘要: We introduce a mapping between the ground state of interacting fermionic Hamiltonians and the non-equilibrium steady state of a purely dissipative open quantum system. Within the framework of third quantization, we map the Fermi-Hubbard Hamiltonian onto Lindblad jump operators acting on Majorana fermions. Remarkably, both hopping and interaction terms map onto jump operators that preserve the Gaussianity of Majorana fermions along individual quantum trajectories. As a result, the dynamics can be unravelled and each trajectory can be simulated efficiently using only two-point correlation functions, with a computational cost that scales polynomially with system size. We further show that finite particle number requires negative dissipative rates, leading to an intrinsically non-Markovian dynamics. The corresponding trajectory unravelling involves both positive and negative stochastic weights and exhibits a sign problem and large fluctuations, so that convergence requires an exponentially large number of trajectories. The overall computational cost remains exponential in system size despite the efficient Gaussian representation of individual trajectories, but is crucially dependent on the computational complexity of the non-Markovian unravelling, motivating further studies on the efficiency of such unravellings.

</details>

结论: 

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 基于轨迹的动力学系统学习在噪声、混沌或稀疏观测条件下往往较为脆弱，因为微小的逐点误差会迅速放大。我们提出了一种基于转移统计量的系统辨识方法，通过匹配概率质量在数据自适应网格上的诱导运动来学习动力学。给定轨迹数据后，我们构建状态空间的非结构化划分，并用正则化的Ulam转移矩阵近似Perron-Frobenius算子。我们将硬单元指示函数替换为连续的分片光滑单位分解权重，从而得到一个支持对所学向量场参数进行基于梯度优化的马尔可夫矩阵。这使得两种相关的训练目标成为可能：通过转移矩阵的平稳特征向量匹配不变测度，以及匹配完整转移矩阵以捕获状态空间区域间的输运行为。在Lorenz-63、Lorenz-96以及降阶NOAA海表温度预测问题上的数值实验表明，转移统计量匹配比逐点轨迹匹配能给出更可靠的长时间动力学，尤其在测量噪声和稀疏采样条件下表现突出。该方法为学习混沌和部分观测动力学系统提供了一种稳健的算子理论替代方案，以取代轨迹级损失函数。

英文原始摘要: Trajectory-based learning of dynamical systems is often fragile in the presence of noise, chaos, or sparse observations, as small pointwise errors can rapidly amplify. We introduce a transition-statistics approach to system identification that learns dynamics by matching the induced motion of probability mass across a data-adaptive mesh. Given trajectory data, we build an unstructured partition of state space and approximate the Perron--Frobenius operator with a regularized Ulam transition matrix. We replace hard cell indicators with continuous, piecewise-smooth partition-of-unity weights, yielding a Markov matrix supporting gradient-based optimization with respect to the parameters of a learned vector field. This enables two related training objectives: matching invariant measures through the stationary eigenvectors of the transition matrices, and matching the full transition matrices to capture transport between regions of state space. Numerical experiments on Lorenz-63, Lorenz-96, and a reduced-order NOAA sea surface temperature forecasting problem show that transition-statistics matching gives more reliable long-time dynamics than pointwise trajectory matching, particularly under measurement noise and sparse sampling. The approach provides a robust operator-theoretic alternative to trajectory-level losses for learning chaotic and partially observed dynamical systems.

</details>

中文摘要: 本文讨论了非线性动态优化问题的求解，此类问题通过计算最优操控输入分布来强制实现期望的输出分布。这类轨迹优化问题常见于化工过程应用，例如在间歇过程中计算最优温度或进料分布（针对补料间歇过程），以强制实现时变的产品质量分布，从而紧密控制反应速率或热生成速率。我们提出了深度神经算子来近似函数到函数的映射，作为此类动态优化问题求解的代理模型。我们特别采用深度算子网络和傅里叶增强的DeepONet，在一个间歇聚合反应器案例研究中，通过最优温度程序强制实现数均分子量和重均分子量分布以及最终转化率目标。结果表明，傅里叶增强的DeepONet架构在近似不同实例的动态优化问题解方面表现优异，相比标准DeepONet架构和标准前馈神经网络实现了更低的预测误差。

英文原始摘要: This paper addresses the solution of nonlinear dynamic optimization problems that compute optimal manipulated input profiles to enforce desired output profiles. Such trajectory optimization problems commonly arise in chemical process applications, for example, batch processes where optimal temperature or feeding profiles (in case of fed-batch processes) are calculated to enforce time-varying product quality profiles, tightly controlling the reaction rate or rate of heat generation. We propose deep neural operators that approximate function to function mappings as surrogates for the solution of such dynamic optimization problems. We specifically employ deep operator networks (DeepONets) and Fourier-enhanced DeepONets in a batch polymerization reactor case study for which number-average and weight-average molecular weight profiles, together with a final conversion target, are enforced through an optimal temperature program. Our results show that the Fourier-enhanced DeepONet architecture performs very well in approximating the solution of the dynamic optimization problem for different instances, achieving a lower prediction error compared to the standard DeepONet architecture and standard feedforward neural networks.

</details>

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们研究在过程不确定性和测量不确定性下连续时间随机系统的基于采样的运动规划问题，并对安全性和性能提供概率保证。机器人动力学被建模为连续时间线性随机微分方程，而传感器测量在离散时刻到达。我们推导了一个离线混合信念传播模型，其中信念在测量之间按连续时间常微分方程演化，并在测量时刻经历离散卡尔曼滤波更新跳变。为确保安全，我们引入了基于信念障碍函数的安全检查器，用于段级概率验证。这使得规划器能够对整条连续轨迹段进行安全认证，并检测传统基于节点的检查所遗漏的样本间机会约束违例。这些组件共同提供了一个原则性的基于采样信念规划框架，同时考虑了连续时间不确定性传播和连续时间安全要求。我们将该方法与RRT和SST规划器集成，并在多个基准环境中进行了评估。结果表明，所提方法实现了高成功率和机会约束的稳健执行，包括在离散时间方法因遗漏样本间不安全行为而失败的窄通道场景中同样有效。

英文原始摘要: We address sampling-based motion planning for continuous-time stochastic systems under process and measurement uncertainty, with probabilistic guarantees on safety and performance. The robot dynamics are modeled as a continuous-time linear stochastic differential equation, while sensor measurements arrive at discrete time instants. We derive an offline hybrid belief propagation model in which the belief evolves according to continuous-time ODEs between measurements and undergoes discrete Kalman filter update jumps at measurement times. To ensure safety, we introduce a belief-barrier-function-based safety checker for segment-level probabilistic verification. This enables the planner to certify safety over entire continuous trajectory segments and detect inter-sample chance-constraint violations that are missed by conventional node-based checks. Together, these components provide a principled framework for sampling-based belief planning that accounts for both continuous-time uncertainty propagation and continuous-time safety requirements. We integrate the method with RRT and SST planners and evaluate it across multiple benchmark environments. The results show that the proposed method achieves high success rates and robust enforcement of chance constraints, including in narrow-passage scenarios where discrete-time counterparts fail due to missed inter-sample unsafe behavior.

</details>

*Yiteng Yuan,Junyan Wang,Zheyuan Liu,Hong Jia,Lei Fan,Zhulin Tao,Lianbo Guo*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

主要创新点: 提出基于线性插值路径的流匹配框架，用观测视图与缺失视图之间的确定性ODE概率流替代扩散模型的随机轨迹，并整合簇级和基于熵的对齐机制强制跨视图聚类一致性。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

结论: 确定性流匹配框架在IMVC任务中优于扩散方法，建立了新的最优性能。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 不完整多视图聚类（IMVC）旨在解决某些视图缺失时的多模态数据聚类问题。近年来，端到端生成方法利用扩散模型通过随机噪声到数据的轨迹来恢复缺失视图。然而，这类机制虽然表达能力较强，但并未明确为聚类而设计，因为它们从与聚类无关的噪声进行初始化，并依赖随机去噪动态。本文重新审视端到端生成式IMVC中的概率路径设计。我们引入了一个流匹配框架，在配对视图表示之间使用线性插值路径，用观测视图与缺失视图之间的概率流替代扩散。我们提供了形式化分析，表明确定性ODE流在本质上比基于扩散的随机轨迹更好地与聚类目标对齐，尤其是在尊重类条件数据分布的传输机制以及在有限步机制下保持聚类一致性方面。基于这一洞察，我们开发了一种端到端IMVC架构，将直线路径流匹配视图补全与簇级和基于熵的对齐相结合，以强制跨视图聚类一致性。在标准IMVC基准上的大量实验表明，所提出的框架建立了新的最优性能。

英文原始摘要: Incomplete Multi-View Clustering addresses the problem of clustering multi-modal data when certain views are missing. Recent end-to-end generative approaches leverage diffusion models to recover missing views via stochastic noise-to-data trajectories. While expressive, such mechanisms are not explicitly designed for clustering, as they initialize from cluster-agnostic noise and rely on stochastic denoising dynamics. In this work, we revisit probability path design in end-to-end generative IMVC. We introduce a flow-matching framework with a linear interpolation path between paired view representations, that replaces diffusion with probability flows between observed and missing views. We provide a formal analysis showing that deterministic ODE flows are inherently better aligned with clustering objectives than diffusion-based stochastic trajectories, especially in terms of transport mechanisms that respect class-conditional data distributions and maintain cluster consistency in finite-step regimes. Building upon this insight, we develop an end-to-end IMVC architecture that integrates straight-path flow-matching view completion with cluster-level and entropy-based alignment to enforce cross-view clustering consistency. Extensive experiments on standard IMVC benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework establishes new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: McMg在迭代框架中将残差映射到校正量，结合线性多通道转移算子、局部自适应模板、神经PDE算子和介质相关光滑器；非线性物理特征在设置阶段预计算并缓存，在线迭代退化为固定系数卷积；LLPF策略通过逐层添加粗网格层级并仅微调新参数实现大区域扩展。

主要结果: McMg比强经典基线方法需要显著更少的迭代次数和运行时间，且持续优于现有神经预条件子；小区域训练的模型可直接迁移到更大区域和更高有效波数。

结论: 通过在通道维度保留局部波信息并辅以LLPF跨尺度策略，McMg有效解决了高波数Helmholtz方程中传统多重网格的信息丢失问题，在效率和解质量上均优于经典和神经基线方法。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 求解高波数异质Helmholtz方程仍然具有挑战性，因为离散算子是不定的，污染效应会降低相位精度，且标量粗网格校正会丢弃振荡误差所携带的局部相位和传播方向信息。我们提出了多通道多重网格（McMg），一种用于异质Helmholtz方程的学习型相空间多重网格预条件子。McMg不直接预测解，而是在迭代框架内将残差映射到校正量。其核心思想是在对物理空间进行粗化的同时在通道维度保留未解的局部波信息：每个粗网格节点携带一个学习到的振幅、相位、方向和散射系数包，而非单一标量未知量。该架构结合了线性多通道转移算子、局部自适应模板、神经PDE算子以及由波速生成系数的介质相关光滑器。对于固定介质，V-循环对残差是线性的；非线性物理特征在设置阶段计算一次并缓存，因此每次在线迭代简化为固定系数的卷积。我们进一步研究了跨尺度泛化性。在小区域上训练的模型可直接迁移到更大区域和更高有效波数，逐层渐进微调（LLPF）策略通过添加新的粗网格层级并仅微调新引入的参数来改善大区域可扩展性。在高频、高对比度和大规模三维问题上的数值实验表明，McMg比强经典基线方法需要显著更少的迭代次数和运行时间，同时持续优于现有的神经预条件子。

英文原始摘要: Solving heterogeneous Helmholtz equations at high wavenumbers remains challenging because the discretized operator is indefinite, pollution degrades phase accuracy, and scalar coarse-grid correction can discard the local phase and propagation-direction information carried by oscillatory errors. We propose Multi-channel Multigrid (McMg), a learned phase-space multigrid preconditioner for heterogeneous Helmholtz equations. Rather than predicting the solution directly, McMg maps residuals to corrections within an iterative framework. Its central idea is to coarsen physical space while retaining unresolved local wave information in the channel dimension: each coarse node carries a learned packet of amplitude, phase, direction, and scattering coefficients rather than a single scalar unknown. The architecture combines linear multi-channel transfer operators with locally adaptive stencils, neural PDE operators, and medium-dependent smoothers whose coefficients are generated from the wave speed. For a fixed medium, the V-cycle is linear in the residual; nonlinear physical features are computed once in a setup phase and cached, so each online iteration reduces to convolutions with fixed coefficients. We further study generalization across scales. Models trained on small domains transfer directly to larger domains and higher effective wavenumbers, and a Layer-by-Layer Progressive Finetuning (LLPF) strategy improves large-domain scalability by adding new coarse levels while finetuning only the newly introduced parameters. Numerical experiments on high-frequency, high-contrast, and large-scale three-dimensional problems demonstrate that McMg requires substantially fewer iterations and less wall-clock time than strong classical baselines, while consistently outperforming existing neural preconditioners.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: LiNO基于第二代小波提升方案，通过对提升变换进行参数化从数据中学习多分辨率分解；在提升的多分辨率空间中，分别演化粗略系数和方向细节系数，实现尺度感知的物理建模；提升变换构造上精确可逆，保证信息保持。

主要结果: LiNO在上述具有挑战性的基准测试中相较于最先进的神经算子展现出优越的性能。

结论: 自适应多分辨率算子为科学机器学习提供了有前景的研究方向，能够有效建模具有多尺度特征的复杂物理系统。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 近年来，神经算子在直接从数据中学习微分方程解算子方面展现出了令人瞩目的成果。该框架学习从参数场到解场的泛函映射，能够预测一整类解而非特定实例。然而，现有算子通常难以同时捕捉全局动力学和精细尺度结构。为了设计能够表示多尺度特征的有效算子，需要一个分层多尺度分解框架。在本研究中，我们开发了提升神经算子（LiNO），这是一种基于第二代小波提升方案构建的多分辨率算子。LiNO通过对提升变换进行参数化，直接从数据中学习多分辨率分解。该提升变换对底层解函数具有自适应性，且在构造上精确可逆，从而实现了信息保持的多尺度算子学习。在提升的多分辨率空间中，算子分别演化粗略系数和方向细节系数，实现了对底层物理的尺度感知建模。我们在多个基准测试上评估了LiNO，包括达西流、泊松方程、Allen-Cahn方程、可压缩Navier-Stokes方程以及Gray-Scott反应扩散系统。这些基准测试涵盖了广泛的物理行为，包括多尺度现象、输运主导动力学和混沌系统。与最先进的神经算子相比，LiNO在这些具有挑战性的基准测试上表现出色。这些结果表明，自适应多分辨率算子为科学机器学习提供了有前景的方向。

英文原始摘要: Recently, neural operators have shown promising outcomes for learning solution operators of differential equations directly from data. This framework learns a functional mapping from the parameter field to the solution field, enabling the prediction of an entire class of solutions rather than a specific instance. However, existing operators often struggle to capture both global dynamics and fine-scale structure simultaneously. To design an effective operator capable of representing multiscale features, a hierarchical multiscale decomposition framework is required. In this study, we develop the Lifting Neural Operator (LiNO), a multiresolution operator built on the second-generation wavelet lifting scheme. LiNO learns a multiresolution decomposition directly from data by parameterizing the lifting transform. This lifting transformation is adaptive to the underlying solution function and exactly invertible by construction, enabling information-preserving multiscale operator learning. In the lifted multiresolution space, the operator evolves coarse and directional detail coefficients separately, resulting in scale-aware modeling of the underlying physics. We evaluate LiNO on several benchmarks, including Darcy flow, the Poisson equation, the Allen-Cahn equation, the compressible Navier-Stokes equation, and the Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion system. Together, these benchmarks cover a wide range of physical behaviors, including multiscale phenomena, transport-dominated dynamics, and chaotic systems. LiNO demonstrates strong performance on these challenging benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art neural operators. These results suggest that adaptive multiresolution operators provide a promising direction for scientific machine learning.

</details>

结论: U3DWind为系统评估低空交通场景中的风诱导影响提供了开放基准，支撑城市空域管理和数据驱动的高保真城市流场模拟研究。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 城市空中交通（UAM）需要对低空风场危害进行可靠评估，因为风、阵风以及建筑诱导的湍流已被认为是影响飞行器稳定性、航线可行性、垂直起降场选址和空域管理的关键因素。尽管风洞实验、计算流体力学（CFD）、多尺度降尺度、降阶模型和无人机规划数据集已推动了风场感知分析的发展，但面向数据驱动的城市级UAM规划的公开资源在地理覆盖范围、场景多样性、垂直延伸、建筑真实性和面向任务的基准测试方面仍然有限。为填补这一空白，我们引入了U3DWind，一个基于GPU加速的格子玻尔兹曼方法-大涡模拟（LBM-LES）框架快速模拟城市流场而生成的建筑解析低空风场数据集。U3DWind覆盖中国五大城市：北京、上海、广州、深圳和香港。它包含720组模拟，每个城市有16个入流方向、三个参考风速和三个季节性大气场景（全年、夏季和冬季）。在10米网格分辨率下，该数据集提供三维三分量（3D3C）速度、湍动能（TKE）、流场密度和流固掩膜。为支持与运营相关的评估，我们进一步定义了五个基线任务：风场预测、稀疏传感器风场重建、选址风暴露排序、适航风合规风险评分和噪声传播建模。作为一个多城市、建筑解析的三维城市风场数据集，U3DWind能够系统评估低空交通场景中风诱导的影响，并为城市空域管理和数据驱动的高保真城市流场模拟提供开放基准。

英文原始摘要: Urban Air Mobility (UAM) requires reliable assessment of low-altitude wind hazards, because winds, gusts, and building-induced turbulence have been recognized as critical factors affecting vehicle stability, route feasibility, vertiport siting, and airspace management. While wind-tunnel experiments, computational fluid dynamics (CFD), multiscale downscaling, reduced-order models, and UAV planning datasets have advanced wind-aware analysis, public resources for data-driven, city-scale UAM planning remain limited in geographic coverage, scenario diversity, vertical extent, building realism, and task-oriented benchmarking. To address this gap, we introduce U3DWind, a building-resolved low-altitude wind-field dataset generated using our GPU-accelerated Lattice Boltzmann Method--Large-Eddy Simulation (LBM-LES) framework for rapid urban flow simulation. U3DWind covers five megacities in China: Beijing, Shanghai, Guangzhou, Shenzhen, and Hong Kong. It contains 720 simulations, with 16 inflow directions, three reference wind speeds, and three seasonal atmospheric scenarios (annual, summer, and winter) for each city. At a 10 m grid resolution, the dataset provides three-dimensional three-component (3D3C) velocity, turbulent kinetic energy (TKE), flow density, and fluid--solid masks. To support operationally relevant evaluation, we further define five baseline tasks: wind-field prediction, sparse-sensor wind-field reconstruction, site wind-exposure ranking, airworthiness wind-compliance risk scoring, and noise propagation modeling. As a multi-city, building-resolved 3D urban wind-field dataset, U3DWind enables systematic evaluation of wind-induced impacts in low-altitude traffic scenarios and provides an open benchmark for urban airspace management and data-driven high-fidelity urban flow simulation.

</details>

主要结果: 获得了涵盖五项核心性质的综合数据集；t-SNE分析显示不同盐化学存在部分聚类、性质随浓度和温度呈连续梯度变化、数据具有物理自洽性，溶剂组成被识别为关键性能调控因素。

结论: 精确的可迁移力场与大规模电解质数据集共同为离子电解质的数据驱动理性设计提供了坚实基础。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 锂离子电池离子电解质的理性设计受限于庞大的溶剂-盐组合空间和经验试错法的低效性。虽然分子动力学（MD）能够桥接微观溶剂化结构与宏观物理化学性质，但经典力场对多组分体系往往缺乏足够的精度。为应对这些挑战，我们开发了一种面向通用离子电解质的自动化可微分OPLS-AA力场参数化工作流。该工作流采用拓扑引导的原子类型化以减少参数冗余，并通过DMFF框架优化Lennard-Jones参数，以实验密度为拟合目标，以离子电导率为独立验证指标。严格的收敛测试产生了一套标准化模拟方案，采用约10万个原子的体系和35-40 ns的NVT运行，以确保输运性质定量的可靠性。在天穹平台上对涵盖67种溶剂和15种锂盐的超过10000种配方进行了高通量MD模拟，生成了涵盖密度、介电常数、粘度、扩散系数和离子电导率五个核心性质的综合数据集。t-SNE可视化揭示了不同盐化学的部分聚类特征、随浓度和温度变化的连续性质梯度以及内在物理自洽性，并发现溶剂组成是另一个关键性能调控因素。总之，精确的可迁移力场和大规模数据集为离子电解质的数据驱动理性设计提供了坚实基础。

英文原始摘要: The rational design of ionic electrolytes for lithium-ion batteries (LIBs) is severely constrained by the vast solvent-salt combinatorial space and low efficiency of empirical trial-and-error. While molecular dynamics (MD) bridges microscopic solvation structures and macroscopic physicochemical properties, classical force fields often lack sufficient accuracy for multicomponent systems. To address these challenges, we develop an automated differentiable OPLS-AA force field parameterization workflow tailored for general ionic electrolytes. It employs topology-guided atom typification to reduce parameter redundancy and optimizes Lennard-Jones parameters via the DMFF framework, with experimental density as the fitting target and ionic conductivity as an independent validation metric. Rigorous convergence tests yield a standardized simulation protocol with $\sim$100,000-atom systems and 35-40 ns NVT runs to ensure reliable transport property quantification. High-throughput MD simulations of over 10,000 formulations spanning 67 solvents and 15 lithium salts are conducted on the Tianqiong platform, generating a comprehensive dataset covering five core properties: density, dielectric constant, viscosity, diffusion coefficient, and ionic conductivity. t-SNE visualization reveals partial clustering of distinct salt chemistries, continuous property gradients with concentration and temperature, and internal physical self-consistency, with solvent composition identified as another key performance regulator. Together, the accurate transferable force field and large-scale dataset provide a solid foundation for data-driven rational design of ionic electrolytes.

</details>

结论: 针对包含奇异广义随机哈密顿系统在内的退化扩散，本文通过解析方法与广义狄利克雷形式严密建立了其长时间遍历行为和弱解的适定性理论。

英文原始摘要: We establish $L^2$-exponential strong ergodicity (strong mixing) with an explicit rate of convergence for a class of degenerate diffusions with multiplicative noise and with singular drift in both the noisy and noise-free components. This class includes diffusions with an additional inert drift given by the gradient of a singular potential, as well as singular generalized stochastic Hamiltonian systems. Cases in which the diffusion is confined to a proper, bounded or unbounded subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d_1+d_2}$ are included. Concrete examples of admissible potentials are provided. To obtain these results, we use an analytical approach and study the long-time behavior of the strongly continuous contraction semigroup generated by the formal Kolmogorov backward operator. Using the theory of generalized Dirichlet forms, these objects are then identified with the transition semigroup and generator of the unique weak solution to the original stochastic differential equation. The existence and uniqueness of this solution are established under near-minimal conditions.
