中文摘要: 质子-反质子衍射解离中的CP偏置可以从多种哲学视角审视，特别是随机实在论和随机认识论。结合时间对称性假设，随机实在论表明物理定律允许热力学反系统的可能性，尽管反系统特性是否能在自然界中物理实现仍是一个开放问题。本文将该偏置解释为表观的，表明存在显著的非幺正贡献，并与源自幺正哈密顿动力学的基本CP破坏相区分。这种表观效应可能源于内禀随机性、环境相互作用或两者之间的干涉。本研究调查了相关机制，并确定了产生、传输或屏蔽CP偏置的条件。在环境分支中，平衡辐射场可能从物质主导的环境中传输CP偏置，尽管因果性约束可能限制这一可能性。在内禀分支中，观测到的偏置与CPT不变性所要求的次主导反系统效应一致。需要进一步的实验来区分这些机制。

英文原始摘要: CP bias in proton--antiproton diffractive dissociation can be viewed from several philosophical perspectives, notably stochastic realism and stochastic epistemicism. When combined with the presumption of temporal symmetry, stochastic realism suggests that the laws of physics allow the possibility of thermodynamic antisystems, although whether antisystemic characteristics can be physically realised in nature remains an open question.
  Here, this bias is interpreted as apparent, indicating significant non-unitary contributions and a distinction from fundamental CP violations arising from unitary Hamiltonian dynamics. Such apparent effects may arise from intrinsic stochasticity, from environmental interactions, or from interference between the two. This work investigates the relevant mechanisms and determines conditions for creating, transmitting, or screening a CP bias.
  In the environmental branch, an equilibrated radiation bath may transmit a CP bias from matter-dominated surroundings, although causality constraints may limit this possibility. In the intrinsic branch, the observed bias is consistent with subleading antisystemic effects required by CPT invariance. Further experiments are needed to distinguish between these mechanisms.

</details>

一句话结论: 基于Koopman算子谱理论提出KBK滤波器，将非线性估计转化为Koopman坐标下的Riccati型演化，性能优.

研究问题: 非线性状态估计中，传统方法如扩展Kalman滤波器依赖显式模型线性化，难以在保持非线性结构的同时实现高效估计，且在数据驱动场景下适用性受限。

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: 将非线性估计表述为Mortensen最大似然估计器，解由HJ偏微分方程刻画；用Koopman主特征函数参数化HJ值函数；发展路径积分表述计算主特征函数；引入基于动力学信息和特征线的基构造进行逼近；推导值函数与状态估计逼近的理论误差界。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 本文介绍了Kalman-Bucy-Koopman（KBK）滤波器，这是一种基于Koopman算子谱理论的非线性状态估计新框架。非线性估计问题被表述为最大似然（Mortensen）估计器，其解由Hamilton-Jacobi（HJ）偏微分方程刻画。所提出的KBK滤波器通过用Koopman主特征函数参数化HJ值函数，为该非线性滤波问题提供了一种谱的、算子理论的实现。这一变换将非线性估计问题转化为Koopman坐标下的Riccati型演化，产生了经典Kalman-Bucy滤波器的线性算子类比，同时在原始状态变量中保持非线性结构。我们发展了用于计算Koopman主特征函数的路径积分表述，并引入了一种基于动力学信息、特征线启发的基构造来逼近这些特征函数。推导了值函数和状态估计逼近的理论误差界。仿真结果表明，该方法相比扩展Kalman滤波器具有更好的性能，并展示了KBK框架在无显式模型线性化的数据驱动场景下的工作能力。

英文原始摘要: This paper introduces the Kalman-Bucy-Koopman (KBK) filter, a novel framework for nonlinear state estimation grounded in Koopman operator spectral theory. The nonlinear estimation problem is formulated as a maximum-likelihood (Mortensen) estimator whose solution is characterized by a Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) partial differential equation. The proposed KBK filter provides a spectral, operator-theoretic realization of this nonlinear filtering problem by parameterizing the HJ value function in terms of principal Koopman eigenfunctions. This transformation converts the nonlinear estimation problem into a Riccati-type evolution in Koopman coordinates, yielding a linear-operator analogue of the classical Kalman-Bucy filter while preserving nonlinear structure in the original state variables. We develop a path-integral formulation for computing principal Koopman eigenfunctions and introduce a dynamics-informed, characteristics-inspired basis construction for their approximation. Theoretical error bounds are derived for value-function and state-estimation approximations. Simulation results demonstrate improved performance over the extended Kalman filter and illustrate the ability of the KBK framework to operate in data-driven settings without explicit model linearization.

</details>

主要创新点: 提出SINDy-SENDAI分层多尺度深度学习框架，在潜在空间中通过SINDy模块识别稀疏非线性动力学方程，将深度学习的多尺度信号分离能力与可解释的动力学方程辨识相结合，实现显式的动态表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 电力系统中基于逆变器的可再生能源渗透率不断提高，引入新的动态行为，使机电振荡监测更具挑战性；同时实际部署要求方法具备鲁棒性、可解释性和在多变运行条件下的稳定性能。

主要结果: SINDy-SENDAI在所有测试场景中持续优于Hankel-DMD方法；学习到的潜在动态信息足够充分，能够准确重建和预测原始状态空间中全系统的行为。

结论: 所提出的分层多尺度框架通过结合深度学习与稀疏动力学识别，实现了可解释、鲁棒的机电振荡监测与预测，满足实际部署需求，在合成系统和真实电网数据上均表现出色。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 监测机电振荡对于维持现代电力系统的稳定性至关重要，特别是在基于逆变器的资源（IBR）渗透率不断提高的情况下，这些资源引入了新的动态行为。在本研究中，我们提出了一种基于SINDy-SENDAI算法的分层多尺度框架，用于表征广域测量所捕捉的暂态动态。所提出的深度学习架构能够稳健地分离嵌入在传感器数据中的低频和高频分量，并在潜在空间中引入非线性动力学系统稀疏识别（SINDy）模块来识别简洁的控制方程。与传统的深度学习方法通常产生可解释性有限的黑盒模型不同，所提出的框架学习显式的动态表示，能够实现物理解释、稳定性评估和机电振荡预测。鉴于现代电力系统的社会重要性，所提出的方法专门设计以满足实际部署的关键要求，即鲁棒性、可解释性和在不同运行条件下的稳定性能。该框架首先在双区域Kundur测试系统上进行验证，以传统模态分析作为基准真值，随后在两个真实数据集上进行演示：2016年伊比利亚振荡事件和2021年意大利南部电网的环境测量。结果表明，SINDy-SENDAI持续优于最先进的Hankel-DMD方法，并且学习到的潜在动态信息足够充分，能够准确重建和预测原始状态空间中全系统的行为。

英文原始摘要: Monitoring electromechanical oscillations is crucial for maintaining the stability of modern power systems, particularly in the presence of increasing penetrations of inverter-based resources (IBRs), which introduce new dynamic behaviors. In this work, we propose a hierarchical multiscale framework based on the SINDy-SENDAI algorithm to characterize the transient dynamics captured by wide-area measurements. The proposed deep learning architecture robustly separates low- and high-frequency components embedded in sensor data and incorporates a Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems (SINDy) module in the latent space to identify parsimonious governing equations. In contrast to conventional deep learning approaches that often produce black-box models with limited interpretability, the proposed framework learns an explicit dynamical representation, enabling physical interpretation, stability assessment, and forecasting of electromechanical oscillations. Given the societal importance of modern power systems, the proposed approach is specifically designed to satisfy key requirements for practical deployment, namely robustness, interpretability, and stable performance under diverse operating conditions. The framework is first validated on the two-area Kundur test system using conventional modal analysis as ground truth and subsequently demonstrated on two real-world datasets: the 2016 Iberian oscillatory event and the 2021 ambient measurements from the southern Italian power grid. The results show that SINDy-SENDAI consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art Hankel-DMD method and that the learned latent dynamics are sufficiently informative to accurately reconstruct and predict the behavior of the full system in the original state space.

</details>

中文摘要: 本文研究了由标准Borel概率空间上的保测动力系统建模的随机环境中，与马尔可夫过程相关的马尔可夫上链的长时间统计和小噪声渐近性。我们的第一个结果为此类上链平稳测度的指数混合提供了一个抽象准则，该准则面向SPDE应用，其假设可以直接从先验估计中验证。为了克服由环境引起的非均匀性，我们将广义耦合论证与遍历理论方法相结合。这使我们能够将沿环境的非均匀估计转化为在正密度时间集上的收缩，然后通过引入块间隙计数论证将其升级为全时间收缩。我们的第二个结果在小噪声极限下，为唯一的平稳测度建立了一个具有良好率函数的Freidlin-Wentzell大偏差原理（LDP）。对于上限，允许噪声是退化的，而确定性回拉吸引子可能具有非平凡的动力学。该抽象理论适用于非自治SPDE。我们用两个例子进行了说明：有界域上的二维Navier-Stokes方程和阻尼Sine-Gordon方程，其中确定性强迫和退化加性噪声都依赖于随机环境。

英文原始摘要: This paper studies the long time statistics and small noise asymptotics of Markov cocycles associated with Markov processes in random environments modeled by measure preserving dynamical systems on a standard Borel probability space.
  Our first result provides an abstract criterion for exponential mixing of stationary measures for such cocycles, formulated toward SPDE applications with assumptions that can be verified directly from a priori estimates. To overcome the nonuniformity from the environment, we combine generalized coupling arguments with ergodic theoretic methods. This allows us to convert nonuniform estimates along the environment into contraction on a positive density set of times, and then upgrade this to all time contraction by introducing a block gap-counting argument.
  Our second result establishes a Freidlin--Wentzell large deviation principle(LDP) for the unique stationary measure in the small noise limit with a good rate function. For the upper bound, the noise is allowed to be degenerate, while the deterministic pullback attractor may have nontrivial dynamics.
  The abstract theory applies to nonautonomous SPDEs. We illustrate it with two examples: the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations on bounded domains and damped Sine--Gordon equations, where both the deterministic forcing and the degenerate additive noise depend on the random environment.

一句话结论: 提出分布级奖励微调框架，有效缓解奖励黑客问题并提升生成图像质量与多样性。

研究问题: 传统强化学习在视觉生成中使用样本级奖励函数，容易导致奖励黑客现象，降低图像多样性并产生视觉异常，同时所有样本独立优化易引发模式坍缩。

主要创新点: 提出分布级奖励框架，通过考虑样本的数据分布而非逐样本评估来微调生成模型，并引入子集替换策略高效估计分布级奖励，同时用强化学习优化事后模型合并系数。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 样本级奖励函数会导致奖励黑客、多样性下降和视觉异常，且独立优化各样本易引发模式坍缩，亟需一种考虑数据分布整体信息的奖励机制。

主要方法: 1) 采用分布级奖励替代样本级奖励进行生成模型微调；2) 引入子集替换策略，仅更新生成参考集的一小部分来高效提供奖励信号；3) 利用强化学习优化事后模型合并系数，缓解SDE引入的训练-推理不一致问题。

主要结果: FID-50K显著改善：SiT从8.30降至5.77，EDM2从3.74降至3.52；定性评估表明方法在保持样本多样性的同时提升了感知质量。

结论: 分布级奖励框架能有效缓解奖励黑客和模式坍缩问题，在多种基础模型上显著提升生成质量并保持多样性。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 用于视觉生成的传统强化学习策略通常采用样本级奖励函数，然而这种做法经常导致奖励黑客现象，损害图像多样性并引入视觉异常。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种新颖框架，利用分布级奖励来微调生成模型，从而确保与真实数据分布更好地对齐。与单独评估样本的奖励不同，分布级奖励考虑了样本的数据分布，缓解了所有样本独立地朝同一方向优化时出现的模式坍缩问题。为了克服估计这些奖励的高昂计算成本，我们引入了一种子集替换策略，通过仅更新生成参考集的一小部分子集来高效地提供奖励信号。此外，我们将强化学习应用于事后模型合并系数的优化，可能缓解在常规强化学习实践中引入随机微分方程（SDE）所导致的训练-推理不一致问题。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多种基础模型上显著改善了FID-50K指标，SiT从8.30降至5.77，EDM2从3.74降至3.52。定性评估也证实我们的方法在保持样本多样性的同时增强了感知质量。

英文原始摘要: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>

英文原始摘要: Modeling tethered space systems is critical for advanced orbital operations. Flexible components such as tethers and space nets are integral to these systems but present significant control challenges due to their high dimensional, strongly coupled, and nonlinear dynamics. While data driven methods offer alternative modeling approaches, they frequently struggle with long term predictive stability and spatial generalization. To address this, we propose the Koopman Graph Dynamics (KGD) framework to learn the structural dynamics by integrating the global linear evolution of the Koopman operator with the local topological priors of Graph Neural Networks. Building upon this representation, we develop a KGD based Model Predictive Control strategy for tethered space systems. Subsequently, the ground experiments on flexible tether and space net demonstrate the high precision modeling capabilities of the proposed method. Crucially, the framework exhibits exceptional capacity for spatial transfer without retraining. Models trained exclusively on small configurations successfully predict and control significantly larger, unseen physical scales. Furthermore, the orbit simulations within a physics engine verify the effectiveness of the proposed approach for tethered space systems.

</details>

主要结果: 压力RMSE为1.1 psi（相对误差0.04%），CO2饱和度RMSE为0.0146（相对误差5%）；单GPU训练20分钟，推理不到1分钟；较DeepONet压力误差降低19%，饱和度误差降低7%，速度提升两个数量级。

结论: KL-DNN模型在精度和效率上均显著优于DeepONet，可作为大规模PDE问题的实用且准确的解决方案，支持快速不确定性量化、历史拟合和实时决策。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 训练用于偏微分方程（PDE）主导的大规模问题的算子学习模型面临维数灾难、内存限制和训练数据有限等挑战。这些挑战出现在许多科学和工程应用中，包括地下流动、气候建模和地质碳储存（GCS）。在本研究中，我们提出了一种基于Karhunen-Loeve深度神经网络（KL-DNN）的可扩展算子学习框架，并展示了其在GCS建模中的性能。该模型在包含100个大规模模拟样本的数据集上训练，这些模拟在具有170万网格单元和50个时间步的三维域中进行。KL-DNN方法利用静态属性的低秩奇异值分解和动态压力场的嵌套Karhunen-Loeve展开构建潜在空间，实现了无需子采样或空间粗化的全分辨率预测。KL-DNN模型的压力平均均方根误差（RMSE）为1.1 psi（相对于域内平均压力的0.04%相对误差），CO2饱和度的RMSE为0.0146（相对于羽流内平均饱和度的5%相对误差）。该模型在单个GPU上仅需20分钟训练，与在相同数据集上训练的DeepONet相比，压力误差降低19%，饱和度误差降低7%，速度提升两个数量级。这些结果以及不到一分钟的推理时间，使所提出的模型成为大规模PDE问题的实用且准确的解决方案，能够实现快速不确定性量化、历史拟合和实时决策支持。

英文原始摘要: Training operator-learning models for large-scale problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) is challenging due to the curse of dimensionality, memory constraints, and limited training data. These challenges arise in many scientific and engineering applications, including subsurface flow, climate modeling, and geological carbon storage (GCS). In this work, we propose a scalable operator-learning framework based on the Karhunen-Loeve Deep Neural Network (KL-DNN) and demonstrate its performance for modeling GCS. The model is trained on a dataset comprising 100 samples of large-scale simulations in a three-dimensional domain with 1.7 million cells and 50 time steps. The KL-DNN method constructs latent spaces using low-rank singular value decomposition of static properties and a nested Karhunen-Loeve expansion for dynamic pressure fields, enabling full-resolution predictions without subsampling or spatial coarsening. The KL-DNN model achieves an average root mean square error (RMSE) of 1.1 psi for pressure (0.04% relative error with respect to the average pressure in the domain) and RMSE of 0.0146 for CO2 saturation (5% relative error with respect to the average saturation inside the plume). The model requires 20 minutes of training on a single GPU, representing a 19% reduction in the pressure errors, 7% reduction in the saturation error, and a two-order-of-magnitude speedup compared to DeepONet trained on the same dataset. These results, along with inference time of less than one minute, establish the proposed model as a practical and accurate solution for large-scale PDE problems, enabling rapid uncertainty quantification, history matching, and real-time decision support.

</details>

*Rajat Ghosh*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

一句话结论: 世界模型的功能架构早源于模型降阶与控制领域，其核心障碍在于可验证性而非预测精度。

研究问题: 学习型世界模型在不可失败的物理系统（电力、热控、过程控制）中部署时缺乏可验证性保证，且其与模型降阶（MOR）控制文献之间的历史渊源与方法互补关系未被充分认识。

主要创新点: 跨学科追溯世界模型功能架构在MOR与控制文献中的早期独立发展，并提出统一MOR可验证性与学习型世界模型非线性表征能力的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 当前学习型世界模型被视为自监督学习的新产物，但其关键功能组件（潜状态编码、动作条件、解码、误差验证）数十年前已在控制工程中存在。认识到这一渊源有助于解决学习型世界模型在安全攸关系统中部署时面临的可验证性瓶颈。

主要方法: 追溯三个社区的技术脉络：基于POD的低维湍流模型（潜动力学）、特征脸方法（编码器-解码器与运行时检查）、以及基于测量POD的热控制框架（完整闭环含先验误差界）；进而对比MOR与学习型世界模型各自的优劣势。

实验 / 验证: 本文为观点/综述性论文，未开展具体实验，而是通过历史追溯与跨社区技术对比进行论证分析。

主要结果: MOR传统提供可验证性、物理基础和数据效率；学习型世界模型提供非线性表征、可迁移性和长时域能力；二者具有高度互补性。

结论: 部署世界模型于不可失败系统的核心障碍是可验证性而非预测保真度，未来应发展融合物理基础与可验证性的世界模型，统一MOR与学习型世界模型两条研究脉络。

局限 / 后续问题: 仅基于抽象层面的历史追溯与对比分析，尚未提出具体的统一框架实现或实验验证。

中文摘要: 世界模型——支持动作条件预测与规划的环境压缩潜表征——通常被视为现代自监督学习的产物。本文论证了世界模型的功能架构在数十年前已于模型降阶（MOR）与控制文献中被独立开发、部署和形式化分析，只是使用了不同名称并服务于不同目的：物理系统的实时运行。我们追溯了该架构在三个社区中的发展。基于本征正交分解（POD）构建的低维湍流模型提供了从混沌环境数据中学习到的潜动力学；早期计算机视觉中的特征脸方法提供了编码器-解码器部分，包括一种原始的运行时有效性检查；而面向设施热控制的基于测量的POD框架则组装了完整的闭环——POD系数作为潜状态，对执行器设定值的参数依赖作为动作条件，模态重构作为解码，并且关键地，先验解析误差界作为验证层，证明了模型预测在闭环中何时可被信任。随后，我们考察了各传统所拥有而另一方所缺乏的内容：MOR贡献了可验证性、物理基础和极高的数据效率；学习型世界模型贡献了非线性表征、可迁移性和长时域。我们论证道，将世界模型部署于不可失败的系统（电力、热控、过程控制）中的突出障碍不是预测保真度而是可验证性，并概述了一个统一这两条脉络的、基于物理且可验证的世界模型研究议程。

英文原始摘要: World models -- compressed latent representations of an environment that support action-conditioned prediction and planning -- are typically presented as a product of modern self-supervised learning. This paper argues that the functional anatomy of a world model was independently developed, deployed, and formally analyzed decades earlier in the model-order-reduction (MOR) and control literature, under different names and for a different purpose: the real-time operation of physical systems. We trace the anatomy across three communities. Low-dimensional models of turbulence built on proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) supplied latent dynamics learned from data of a chaotic environment; eigenface methods in early computer vision supplied the encoder-decoder half, including a primitive runtime validity check; and measurement-based POD frameworks for facility thermal control assembled the complete loop -- POD coefficients as latent state, parametric dependence on actuator setpoints as action conditioning, modal reconstruction as decoding, and, critically, a priori analytical error bounds as a verification layer that certified when the model's predictions could be trusted in closed loop. We then examine what each tradition possesses that the other lacks: MOR contributes verification, physical grounding, and extreme data efficiency; learned world models contribute nonlinear representation, transferability, and horizon. We argue that the outstanding obstacle to deploying world models in systems that cannot fail -- power, thermal, process control -- is not predictive fidelity but verifiability, and we outline a research agenda for physics-grounded, verifiable world models that unifies the two lineages.

</details>

结论: 再分配必须打破漂移移动对称性；累进税通过改变分布尾部特性和谱隙驱动的快速收敛实现有效再分配，而比例税本质上是再分配中性的。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 比例财富税对财富分布的作用类似于均匀引力场：它移动Fokker-Planck方程的漂移项而不改变扩散项，在所有有限时间内保持基尼系数不变。使税收不产生扭曲的同一漂移移动对称性也使其无法通过市场渠道实现再分配。再分配需要打破这一对称性。累进税（约束势）将帕累托稳态替换为更薄尾的分布，其基尼系数是累进性参数的封闭形式函数；源汇项（税收资助的转移支付）直接重塑密度。我们将最优再分配表述为Fokker-Planck方程的控制问题，对包括迁移、逃税和投资组合扭曲在内的干预成本进行惩罚。在一般均衡中，税制设计通过总量资本和生产函数产生反馈，产生一个具有累进性递减回报的自洽McKean-Vlasov方程。Fokker-Planck算子的谱隙决定了收敛速度：累进税在政策相关时间尺度内实现再分配，而比例税则依赖于缓慢的人口更替。

英文原始摘要: A proportional wealth tax acts as a uniform gravitational field on the wealth distribution: it shifts the drift of the Fokker-Planck equation without altering the diffusion, preserving the Gini coefficient at all finite times. The same drift-shift symmetry that makes the tax non-distortionary also makes it non-redistributive through the market channel. Redistribution requires breaking this symmetry. A progressive tax (confining potential) replaces the Pareto steady state with a thinner-tailed distribution whose Gini is a closed-form function of the progressivity parameter; source-sink terms (tax-funded transfers) reshape the density directly.
  We formulate optimal redistribution as a control problem for the Fokker-Planck equation, penalising intervention costs including migration, evasion, and portfolio distortion. In general equilibrium the tax design feeds back through aggregate capital and the production function, yielding a self-consistent McKean-Vlasov equation with diminishing returns to progressivity. The spectral gap of the Fokker-Planck operator determines convergence speed: progressive taxes redistribute within policy-relevant timescales, whereas proportional taxes rely on slow demographic turnover.

</details>

结论: b-LOAD是一种可扩展方法，能将碎片化领域知识转化为更可靠的因果效应估计，特别适用于数据稀缺和结构复杂场景。

中文摘要: 局部因果发现是全局结构学习的可扩展替代方案。然而，在数据稀缺场景下，由于有限样本不确定性、不完整的局部邻域和未解决的马尔可夫等价性，它难以识别有效的调整集。尽管许多应用领域提供了结构化的背景知识，但将其整合到局部因果发现中的工作仍然有限。我们提出b-LOAD，这是LOAD算法的知识增强扩展，用于局部发现最优调整集。b-LOAD将先验边约束直接整合到局部结构学习过程中，并利用Meek规则动态扩展发现前沿，从而在相关局部子图上生成一个知识约束的部分有向图。该策略有助于防止由先验知识引入的结构相关节点被局部搜索排除。我们证明，在可靠的背景知识下，该过程单调地细化可容许等价类，并能扩大可识别因果查询的集合，使得恢复仅凭观测条件独立性信息无法识别的最优调整集成为可能。实验表明，与纯数据驱动和标准知识增强基线相比，b-LOAD改善了下游因果效应估计，尤其在数据稀缺和结构复杂的场景下表现突出。在真实生物网络上的结果表明，局部针对性的先验知识提供最大收益，并在中等结构噪声下仍然有益。这些发现使b-LOAD成为将碎片化领域知识转化为更可靠因果效应估计的可扩展方法。

英文原始摘要: Local causal discovery is a scalable alternative to global structure learning. However, it can struggle to identify valid adjustment sets in data-scarce settings because of finite-sample uncertainty, incomplete local neighborhoods, and unresolved Markov equivalence. Although many application domains provide structured background knowledge, its integration into local causal discovery remains limited. We propose b-LOAD, a knowledge-informed extension of the LOAD algorithm for local discovery of optimal adjustment sets. b-LOAD incorporates prior edge constraints directly into the local structure-learning procedure and uses Meek's rules to expand the discovery frontier dynamically, yielding a knowledge-constrained partially directed graph over the relevant local subgraph. This strategy helps prevent structurally relevant nodes introduced by prior knowledge from being excluded by local search. We prove that, under sound background knowledge, the procedure monotonically refines the admissible equivalence class and can enlarge the set of identifiable causal queries, enabling recovery of optimal adjustment sets that are not identifiable from observational conditional-independence information alone. Empirically, b-LOAD improves downstream causal effect estimation relative to purely data-driven and standard knowledge-augmented baselines, particularly in data-scarce and structurally complex regimes. Results on real-world biological networks show that locally targeted prior knowledge provides the largest gains and remains beneficial under moderate structural noise. These findings position b-LOAD as a scalable approach for converting fragmented domain knowledge into more reliable causal-effect estimation.

</details>

主要方法: 推导分数动力学的连续时间极限得到耦合非线性随机微分方程；对$k$-均匀、$d$-正则随机超图采用稀疏退火副本分析，在副本对称假设下求解鞍点方程；利用Fokker-Planck方程分析有限$N$涨落，噪声协方差由超图结构确定；对极限情形进行解析对比。

实验 / 验证: 本文为纯理论研究，未涉及数值实验；通过对$k\to N$、$k\to2$、$d\to\infty$等极限情形的解析分析，验证模型与标准少数者博弈、网络少数者博弈和并行少数者博弈的一致性。

主要结果: 获得了临界曲面$\alpha_{\mathrm{crit}}(k,d)$，标准MG值$0.3374$仅在单超边极限下恢复；推导了全局波动率、可预测性、超边挫败和冻结分数等序参量的表达式及其临界标度行为；给出了重复子稳定性判据和有限$N$涨落的Fokker-Planck方程。

结论: HMG-L框架成功将少数者博弈推广至超图结构，相变行为由超图参数$(k,d)$决定，而非简单地回到全局耦合值；模型在多种极限下退化为已知的MG变体，验证了理论的普适性和一致性。

局限 / 后续问题: 分析限于$k$-均匀、$d$-正则随机超图和副本对称假设；有限$N$涨落分析虽已给出Fokker-Planck方程，但未涉及非平衡动力学或更一般超图拓扑的扩展。

中文摘要: 我们对具有局部超边收益的超图少数者博弈（HMG-L）进行了理论推导。在该模型中，$N$个自适应代理同时在多个重叠群体中竞争，这些群体被建模为静态超图$\mathcal{H}=(\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E})$的超边。每条超边构成一个独立的局部少数者博弈，代理在其所属的所有群体中累积收益。我们推导了分数动力学的连续时间极限，由此得到代理策略极化变量的一组耦合非线性随机微分方程。确定性的漂移项被证明源自一个全局代价函数，该函数将标准少数者博弈哈密顿量推广到超图结构的相互作用。我们对$k$-均匀、$d$-正则随机超图的稳态进行了稀疏退火副本分析，在副本对称假设下获得了鞍点方程、显式的重复子稳定性判据以及用于稀疏修正的Bethe/腔方程。主要的稀疏区域相变发生在临界曲面$\alpha_{\mathrm{crit}}(k,d)$上，而全局耦合少数者博弈的值$\alpha_{\mathrm{crit}}\simeq0.3374$仅在单独的单超边极限下才能恢复。我们推导了序参量的表达式——全局波动率$\sigma^2$、可预测性$\theta$、超边挫败$F_e$和冻结分数$\phi$——并讨论了它们在临界点附近的标度行为。我们给出了控制有限$N$涨落的Fokker-Planck方程，并从超图结构计算了噪声协方差矩阵。极限情形（$k\to N$、$k\to2$、$d\to\infty$）得到了详细分析，建立了与标准少数者博弈、网络少数者博弈和并行少数者博弈模型的联系。

英文原始摘要: We provide a theoretical derivation of the Hypergraph Minority Game with Local Hyperedge Payoffs (HMG-L), in which $N$ adaptive agents compete simultaneously in multiple overlapping groups modeled as hyperedges of a static hypergraph $\Hyper=(\Vset,\Eset)$. Each hyperedge constitutes an independent local minority game, and agents accumulate payoffs across all groups to which they belong. We derive the continuum-time limit of the score dynamics, from which we obtain a set of coupled nonlinear stochastic differential equations for the agents' strategy polarization variables. The deterministic drift is shown to derive from a global cost function that generalizes the standard Minority Game Hamiltonian to hypergraph-structured interactions. We perform a sparse-annealed replica analysis of the stationary state for the case of a $k$-uniform, $d$-regular random hypergraph, obtaining the saddle-point equations within the replica-symmetric ansatz, an explicit replicon stability criterion, and Bethe/cavity equations for sparse corrections. The leading sparse-regime transition occurs on a critical surface $\alphacrit(k,d)$, while the globally coupled MG value $\alphacrit\simeq0.3374$ is recovered only in the separate single-hyperedge limit. We derive expressions for the order parameters -- global volatility $σ^2$, predictability $θ$, hyperedge frustration $F_e$, and frozen fraction $φ$ -- and discuss their scaling behavior near criticality. The Fokker-Planck equation governing finite-$N$ fluctuations is presented, and the noise covariance matrix is computed from the hypergraph structure. Limiting cases ($k\to N$, $k\to2$, $d\to\infty$) are analyzed in detail, establishing connections to the standard MG, networked MG, and parallel MG models.

</details>

研究问题: 在确定性Ricci流下，负热容区域的黑洞度规扰动会增长导致不稳定，而随机涨落能否改变Ricci流几何稳定性与热力学稳定性之间的关联尚不清楚。

主要创新点: 将乘性随机噪声引入Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞度规扰动的Ricci流中，发现强随机性可抑制负热容区域扰动的增长并稳定热力学不稳定构型；同时将动力学重表述为熵变量在自由能景观上的演化。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 探究Ricci流的几何稳定性与黑洞热力学稳定性之间的关联，以及随机涨落对该关联的潜在修正作用。

主要方法: 在Headrick-Wiseman的Ricci流框架中引入含负宇宙学常数的Ricci目标项，对球对称SAdS度规扰动进行确定性及随机Ricci流数值模拟，并将动力学重写为熵变量在自由能景观上的演化，辅以Monte Carlo模拟和Fokker-Planck方程验证。

实验 / 验证: 分别对正、负热容区域进行确定性Ricci流数值模拟；引入不同强度乘性随机噪声后观察扰动演化；通过Monte Carlo模拟和Fokker-Planck方程验证度规流结果。

主要结果: 正热容区域角向扰动弛豫至SAdS不动点；负热容区域扰动在确定性流下增长；足够强的随机噪声可抑制该增长并稳定构型；自由能景观表述与Monte Carlo及Fokker-Planck结果一致。

结论: 随机涨落可以修正渐近AdS黑洞时空中Ricci流几何稳定性与热力学稳定性之间的关系。

局限 / 后续问题: 研究局限于球对称扰动的Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞，更一般的扰动模式及其他黑洞解的适用性有待验证；摘要中未明确随机噪声的物理来源。

中文摘要: 我们研究了Schwarzschild--Anti de Sitter黑洞度规球对称扰动的随机Ricci流。在Headrick和Wiseman的Ricci流分析基础上，我们通过Ricci目标项引入负宇宙学常数，并研究该流与黑洞热力学热容之间的关联。数值模拟表明，在正热容区域，度规角向扇区的扰动向Schwarzschild--Anti de Sitter不动点弛豫；而在负热容区域，这些扰动在确定性Ricci流下增长。随后我们引入乘性随机噪声，发现足够强的随机性可以抑制这些扰动的增长，从而有效稳定原本热力学不稳定的构型。最后，我们将动力学重新表述为熵变量在热力学自由能景观上的演化，并通过Monte Carlo模拟及相关的Fokker--Planck方程支持了度规流的结果。这些结果表明，随机涨落可以改变渐近Anti de Sitter黑洞时空中Ricci流下的几何稳定性与热力学稳定性之间的关系。

英文原始摘要: We investigate the stochastic Ricci flow of spherically symmetric perturbations of the Schwarzschild--Anti de Sitter black-hole metric. Elaborating on the Ricci-flow analysis of Headrick and Wiseman, we include a negative cosmological constant through a Ricci-target term and study how the flow is correlated with the thermodynamic heat capacity of the black hole. Numerical simulations show that, in the positive heat-capacity regime, perturbations of the angular sector of the metric relax toward the Schwarzschild--Anti de Sitter fixed point, while in the negative heat-capacity regime they grow under the deterministic Ricci flow. We then introduce a multiplicative stochastic noise and find that sufficiently strong stochasticity can suppress the growth of these perturbations, effectively stabilizing configurations that would otherwise be thermodynamically unstable. Finally, we reformulate the dynamics in terms of an entropy variable evolving on a thermodynamic free-energy landscape, and support the metric-flow results through Monte Carlo simulations and the associated Fokker--Planck equation. These results suggest that stochastic fluctuations can modify the relation between geometric stability under Ricci flow and thermodynamic stability in asymptotically Anti de Sitter black-hole spacetimes.

</details>

*George Coote,Matthew J. Colbrook*

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29083v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.29083v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Residual-Guided+Dictionary+Learning+for+Spectrally+Accurate+Koopman+Approximation++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 以谱可靠性为目标训练神经Koopman字典，通过残差认证和条件数约束减少谱污染。

研究问题: 数值Koopman谱虽易于计算却难以信赖——有限EDMD矩阵的特征值可能与无穷维Koopman算子的真实谱无关，现有方法缺乏对谱可靠性的保障。

主要创新点: 将谱可靠性作为字典学习的显式优化目标，通过最小化Residual DMD残差来认证谱对象的真实性，同时惩罚提升数据矩阵的条件数以保证数值稳定性，将小残差与良态表示耦合在统一损失中。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 现有神经Koopman学习方法通常仅以预测精度为训练目标，忽略了计算出的特征值和模态可能并非真实Koopman谱对象这一根本问题，导致谱污染严重、谱声明不可信。

主要方法: 训练神经网络字典以最小化Residual DMD残差（算子级后验误差，用于检验特征值和模态是否为真实Koopman谱对象），同时在损失函数中惩罚提升数据矩阵的条件数以防止坐标系坍缩，将谱残差最小化与良态表示两个要求统一在同一个优化目标中。

实验 / 验证: 在保守和耗散基准动力系统以及真实海表温度数据上进行了测试，与标准固定字典方法进行对比，评估谱污染程度、残差伪谱包含性、预测误差及含噪观测下的一步预测性能。

主要结果: 在基准系统上显著减少谱污染、改善残差伪谱包含性并降低预测误差；在海表温度数据上获得更清晰的Koopman诊断，并在无控制方程条件下从含噪观测中获得显著更优的一步预测。

结论: 神经Koopman学习不应仅以预测精度评判，而应以谱声明是否可被认证为标准；Residual DMD残差提供谱认证，条件数约束保证该认证在计算上可行。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: Koopman理论承诺在非线性动力学中提供线性结构，但数值Koopman谱易于计算却难以信赖。有限EDMD矩阵总有特征值；问题在于其中许多可能与无穷维算子无关。在本文中，我们将谱可靠性作为字典学习的目标。我们训练神经网络字典不仅为了预测下一个快照，而是最小化残差动态模式分解残差：这是一种算子级后验误差，用于检验计算出的特征值和模态是否为真正的Koopman谱对象。为防止学习的观测函数坍缩到不稳定的坐标系中，损失函数还惩罚提升数据矩阵的条件数。因此，该方法将两个不应分离的要求耦合在一起：小的Koopman残差和良态表示。结果是学到的字典具有表达力强、数值稳定和谱纪律性好的特点。在保守和耗散基准系统上，该方法显著减少了谱污染，改善了残差伪谱包含性，并相比标准固定字典降低了预测误差。在海表温度数据上，它给出了更清晰的Koopman诊断，并在无控制方程的情况下从含噪观测中获得了显著更好的一步预测。核心信息很简单：神经Koopman学习不应仅凭预测来评判，而应看其谱声明是否可被认证。残差提供认证；条件数保证其可计算。

英文原始摘要: Koopman theory promises linear structure in nonlinear dynamics, but numerical Koopman spectra are easy to compute and hard to trust. A finite EDMD matrix always has eigenvalues; the problem is that many of them may have nothing to do with the infinite-dimensional operator. In this paper we make spectral reliability the objective of dictionary learning. We train neural-network dictionaries not merely to predict the next snapshot, but to minimize Residual Dynamic Mode Decomposition residuals: operator-level a posteriori errors that test whether computed eigenvalues and modes are genuine Koopman spectral objects. To keep the learned observables from collapsing into an unstable coordinate system, the loss also penalizes the condition number of the lifted data matrix. Thus the method couples two requirements that should not be separated: small Koopman residuals and a well-conditioned representation. The result is a learned dictionary that is expressive, numerically stable, and spectrally disciplined. Across conservative and dissipative benchmark systems, the method sharply reduces spectral pollution, improves residual pseudospectral inclusion, and lowers forecast error relative to standard fixed dictionaries. On sea-surface temperature data, it gives cleaner Koopman diagnostics and substantially better one-step forecasts from noisy observations with no governing equations. The message is simple: neural Koopman learning should be judged not by prediction alone, but by whether its spectral claims can be certified. Residuals provide the certificate; conditioning makes it computable.

</details>

*Alex Colagrande,Paul Caillon,Eva Feillet,Alexandre Allauzen*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28122v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.28122v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Higher-Order+Fourier+Neural+Operator%3A+Explicit+Mode+Mixer+for+Nonlinear+PDEs++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出高阶谱卷积将FNO从对角调制扩展为显式n线性模态混合，显著提升非线性PDE求解性能。

研究问题: FNO的谱卷积基于对角调制假设，适用于傅里叶基可对角化算子的线性常系数PDE，但非线性PDE中模态间存在由多项式非线性控制的结构化交互，FNO缺乏捕捉这种交互的归纳偏置。

主要创新点: 提出高阶谱卷积（Higher-Order Spectral Convolution），将FNO的对角调制扩展为显式的n线性模态混合，使其与非线性PDE的多项式非线性动力学对齐。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 非线性PDE的傅里叶模态之间存在由多项式非线性驱动的结构化交互，而标准FNO仅进行对角调制，无法显式建模这些交互，因此需要额外的归纳偏置来提升非线性PDE的求解能力。

主要方法: 设计高阶谱卷积作为谱混合器，在傅里叶域中实现显式的n线性模态混合，替代FNO原有的对角调制机制，构建高阶FNO（HO-FNO）架构，使其归纳偏置与非线性PDE动力学一致。

实验 / 验证: 在标准基准数据集上，将HO-FNO与其他谱神经算子、最先进的Transformer及状态空间模型进行对比评估，重点测试高度非线性区域（如多项式强迫泊松方程）的性能。

主要结果: HO-FNO保持了FNO架构的效率，持续优于其他谱神经算子；在多个数据集上与最先进的Transformer和状态空间模型性能相当或更优；在高度非线性区域增益尤为显著，单层HO-FNO即可超越多达16层的FNO模型。

结论: 高阶谱卷积通过显式n线性模态混合有效捕捉非线性PDE的结构化模态交互，使HO-FNO在保持计算效率的同时显著提升非线性PDE求解性能。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 神经算子利用深度神经网络学习函数空间之间的映射。其中，傅里叶神经算子（FNO）尤为有效：其谱卷积依赖于低维傅里叶域表示，并且能够处理不同分辨率的输入。这一设计与傅里叶基对角化底层算子的场景高度契合，例如周期域上的线性常系数偏微分方程，其中傅里叶模态独立演化。然而，非线性偏微分方程可能受益于额外的归纳偏置，因为它们展现出由多项式非线性控制的模态间结构化交互。为了捕捉这一归纳偏置，我们提出了高阶谱卷积，这是一种谱混合器，将FNO从对角调制扩展为显式的n线性模态混合，与非线性偏微分方程的动力学相一致。我们在标准基准上的实验表明，所提出的高阶FNO（HO-FNO）保持了基于FNO架构的效率，并持续优于其他谱神经算子。HO-FNO在多个数据集上的表现也与最先进的Transformer和状态空间模型相当或更优，在高度非线性区域中增益更为显著，例如具有多项式强迫项的泊松方程，单层HO-FNO即可超越多达16层的FNO模型。我们已在以下地址开源代码以供复现：https://github.com/AlexColagrande/HO-FNO。

英文原始摘要: Neural operators provide deep neural networks for learning mappings between function spaces. Among them, the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) is particularly effective: its spectral convolution relies on low-dimensional Fourier-domain representations and can handle inputs at different resolutions. This design aligns well with settings where the Fourier basis diagonalizes the underlying operator, such as linear, constant-coefficient PDEs on periodic domains, in which Fourier modes evolve independently. However, nonlinear PDEs may benefit from an additional inductive bias, as they exhibit structured interactions between modes, governed by polynomial nonlinearities. To capture this inductive bias, we introduce the Higher-Order Spectral Convolution, a spectral mixer that extends FNO from diagonal modulation to explicit n-linear mode mixing, aligned with the dynamics of nonlinear PDEs. Our experiments on standard benchmarks show that the proposed Higher-Order FNO (HO-FNO) retains the efficiency of FNO-based architectures and consistently improves over other spectral neural operators. HO-FNO also performs on par with or better than state-of-the-art transformers and state-space models on several datasets, with stronger gains in highly nonlinear regimes, such as the Poisson equation with polynomial forcing, where a single HO-FNO layer outperforms FNO models with up to 16 layers. We open-source our code for reproducibility at: https://github.com/AlexColagrande/HO-FNO.

</details>

*Robin Requadt,Housen Li*

<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: 定义和依赖马尔可夫链模型并建立参数可识别性；定义区分正协作、负协作与独立性的协作性指标并构建一致估计量；对离散含噪聚合数据，基于隐马尔可夫模型开发似然推断方法并解决嵌入问题；提出协作性检验的步降检验方法。

主要结果: 在自然结构条件下证明了潜在动态参数的可识别性；构建了协作性指标的一致估计量；证明了似然推断的一致性与渐近正态性；步降检验具有渐近显著性水平控制和功效保证；模拟与真实数据均表明方法有效。

结论: 所提出的和依赖马尔可夫链框架及配套推断方法能够从聚合信号中有效识别和推断多组分系统的潜在交互作用，具有广泛的适用性和有效性。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 在许多现代实验中，潜在交互作用驱动着多组分随机系统，然而可用的数据仅为聚合测量值，这些测量值掩盖了组分间的依赖关系。这些交互作用能否从聚合信号中被识别和推断，很大程度上仍未被探索。受多通道电生理记录的启发，我们通过引入和依赖马尔可夫链来解决这一问题——这是一类有限状态连续时间多元马尔可夫过程，其转移速率通过聚合状态编码交互作用。在自然的结构条件下，我们建立了从聚合过程中对潜在动态参数的可识别性。我们定义了一个协作性指标，用于区分正协作、负协作和独立性，并构建了其一致估计量。对于离散且含噪声观测的聚合数据，我们通过隐马尔可夫模型开发了基于似然的推断方法，解决了相关的嵌入问题，并证明了一致性和渐近正态性。我们进一步提出了一个用于协作性检验的步降检验方法，具有渐近显著性水平控制和功效保证。模拟和真实数据分析展示了该方法的适用范围和有效性。

英文原始摘要: In many modern experiments, latent interactions drive multicomponent stochastic systems, yet the data are available only as pooled measurements that obscure these dependencies. Whether such interactions can be identified and inferred from aggregate signals remains largely unexplored. Motivated by multi-channel electrophysiological recordings, we address this problem by introducing sum-dependent Markov chains, a class of finite-state continuous-time multivariate Markov processes whose transition rates encode interactions through the aggregate state. Under natural structural conditions, we establish identifiability of the latent dynamic parameters from the aggregate process. We define a cooperativity index that distinguishes positive cooperativity, negative cooperativity and independence, and construct its consistent estimators. For discretely and noisily observed pooled data, we develop likelihood-based inference through a hidden Markov model, address the associated embedding problem, and prove consistency and asymptotic normality. We further propose a stepdown test for cooperativity with asymptotic size control and power guarantees. Simulations and real-data analyses, demonstrate the scope and effectiveness of the methodology.

</details>

*Shijun Cheng,Tariq Alkhalifah*

研究动机: 地震波场模拟是地震学核心任务，但传统FD方法的数值色散和稳定性约束导致计算成本高昂，成为迭代反演工作流的瓶颈，亟需一种能使用更大时间步长且保持精度的波场推进方法。

主要方法: 构建条件扩散模型，以近期波场快照、速度模型和时间步索引为条件直接预测下一时刻波场；采用因果时间加权损失，利用各快照训练误差的指数移动平均作为自适应权重，抑制单步误差在递归展开中的放大。

实验 / 验证: 在Overthrust、SEG/EAGE和Marmousi三个标准速度模型上测试，比较所提方法与GPU加速的十阶交错网格有限差分方法在波场快照、炮集精度和端到端运行时间方面的表现。

主要结果: 所提方法准确再现了波场快照和炮集记录，可使用比FD求解器大10倍的物理时间步长，在相同硬件条件下实现2.17倍端到端加速。

结论: 基于条件扩散的波场传播器能有效突破传统FD稳定性约束，以更大时间步长高效推进地震波场，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算速度，为地震反演工作流提供了更高效的波场模拟途径。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 地震波场模拟是地震学的基础，但传统有限差分（FD）方法仍受数值色散和稳定性约束的限制，通常需要密集的空间网格和很小的时间步长，从而严重限制了迭代反演工作流的有效性。我们提出了一种基于条件扩散的波场传播器，能够从当前时间步递归地推进地震波场到下一时间步。该模型不以学习波场演化的无条件数据分布为目标，而是以最近的波场时间步快照序列、速度模型和波场时间步索引作为条件，使其能够表示相邻物理状态之间的条件转移。通过训练网络直接预测干净的下一波场快照，这种强物理条件化使得可以用单次网络评估替代迭代反向扩散过程来预测每个快照。为提高长时递归展开的稳定性，我们进一步引入了因果时间加权损失，其中自适应权重以每个快照训练误差的指数移动平均累积，强调与正向传播序列一致的训练方向，并减少单步预测误差的放大。由于学习到的传播器与训练快照的时间间隔绑定，而非受限于FD稳定性极限，它可以使用比底层求解器所需大十倍的物理时间步长推进波场。在Overthrust、SEG/EAGE和Marmousi模型上的实验表明，所提方法准确再现了波场快照和炮集记录，并在匹配硬件条件下相比GPU加速的十阶交错网格FD实现了2.17倍的端到端加速。

英文原始摘要: Seismic wavefield simulation is fundamental to seismology, but conventional finite-difference (FD) methods remain limited by numerical dispersion and stability constraints, which often require dense spatial grids and small time steps and thereby severely limit the effectiveness of iterative inversion workflows. We introduce a conditional diffusion-based wavefield propagator that advances seismic wavefields recursively from one time step to the next. Instead of learning an unconditional data distribution of wavefield evolution, the model is conditioned by a short history of recent wavefield time steps (snapshots), the velocity model, and the wavefield time step index, allowing it to represent the conditional transition between adjacent physical states. By training the network to directly predict the clean next wavefield snapshot, this strong physical conditioning makes it possible to replace the iterative reverse diffusion process with a single network evaluation for each predicted snapshot. To improve stability over long recursive rollouts, we further introduce a causal time-weighted loss, in which adaptive weights, accumulated as exponential moving averages of per-snapshot training errors, emphasize training directions that are consistent with the forward propagation sequence and reduce the amplification of one-step prediction errors. Because the learned propagator is tied to the temporal spacing of the training snapshots rather than to the FD stability limit, it can advance the wavefield using a physical time step ten times larger than that required by the underlying solver. Experiments on the Overthrust, SEG/EAGE, and Marmousi models show that the proposed method accurately reproduces wavefield snapshots and shot gathers and achieves an end-to-end speedup of 2.17 x over a GPU-accelerated tenth-order staggered-grid FD implementation under matched hardware conditions.

</details>

主要方法: 1) 设计物理信息谱注意力模块提取工况鲁棒特征，抑制频率偏移导致的感知不确定性；2) 构建不确定性感知自适应图学习机制，依据类尺度高斯分布参数动态调整样本图边权重，缓解不确定性结构传播；3) 开发基于高斯分布的自适应边界损失函数和双准则开集推理策略，优化决策边界并拒绝未知故障。

结论: PGU-OD通过物理信息与不确定性感知的结合，有效解决了旋转机械故障诊断中的开集域泛化问题，在已知和未知故障场景下均表现出优越性能。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 智能工业维护严重依赖于旋转机械的可靠故障诊断。然而，它面临着来自未知故障类型和由不同运行条件引起的域偏移的严峻挑战，这在形式上被定义为开集域泛化（OSDG）问题。现有方法主要是数据驱动的，因此忽略了在特征提取、拓扑学习和决策阶段之间不确定性的级联传播。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了PGU-OD，一种新颖的基于物理信息的图学习框架，具有不确定性感知能力，用于开集域泛化。首先，它设计了一个物理信息谱注意力模块来提取对工况鲁棒的故障特征，从而抑制由频率偏移引起的感知不确定性。进一步，它构建了一个不确定性感知的自适应图学习机制，在类尺度高斯分布参数的引导下动态调整样本图的边权重，以缓解不确定性的结构传播。最后，开发了基于高斯分布的自适应边界损失函数和双准则开集推理策略，以优化决策边界并可靠地拒绝未知故障。在两个公开且广泛使用的旋转机械故障数据集上的大量实验评估表明，所提出的PGU-OD在域偏移下的已知故障分类和未知故障拒绝方面均优于最先进的基线方法。

英文原始摘要: Intelligent industrial maintenance critically relies on reliable fault diagnosis of rotating machinery. However, it faces formidable challenges from unknown fault types and domain shifts induced by varying operating conditions, which is formally formulated as the open-set domain generalization (OSDG) problem. Existing methods are mainly data-driven, thereby overlooking the cascaded propagation of uncertainty across feature extraction, topological learning, and decision-making stages.To tackle this challenge, we propose PGU-OD, a novel Physics-Informed Graph Learning framework with Uncertainty Awareness for Open-set Domain generalization. First, it designs a physics-informed spectral attention module to extract condition-robust fault features, thereby suppressing perceptual uncertainty caused by frequency shifts. Further, it constructs an uncertainty aware adaptive graph learning mechanism to dynamically adjust the edge weights of the sample graph guided by class-scale Gaussian distribution parameters, which mitigates the structural propagation of uncertainty. Finally, a Gaussian-distribution-based adaptive boundary loss function and a dual-criteria open-set inference strategy are developed to optimize decision boundaries and reliably reject unknown faults. Extensive experimental evaluations on two public and widely used rotating machinery fault datasets demonstrate that the proposed PGU-OD outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both known fault classification and unknown fault rejection under domain shifts.

</details>

英文原始摘要: These notes give a proof-oriented introduction to diffusion models from the viewpoint of sampling, tracing a single arc from classical sampling dynamics to modern diffusion samplers, their error analysis, and inference-time control. Throughout, the material is layered into core definitions and identities proved in full, representative estimates proved under simplifying assumptions, and research-level theorems stated with a proof roadmap. The intended audience is beginning graduate students with a background in probability but no prior exposure to stochastic differential equations, stochastic numerics, or diffusion models.

### [56] [PRONE: Petrov-Galerkin Operator Learning Unifies DMD, SINDy & Koopmanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27982v1)
*Matthew J. Colbrook,April Herwig,J. Nathan Kutz*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI:

一句话结论: 提出PRONE框架，用奇异模替代本征模统一多种动力学回归方法，以更少参数超越深度学习算子。

研究问题: 传统数据驱动动力学要求将字典映射到自身来线性化非线性系统，这一人为约束限制了模型灵活性，且使得本征模不再适合作为分析对象。

主要创新点: 提出PRONE框架，使用不同的试验字典和测试字典进行Petrov-Galerkin回归，放弃字典自映射约束，以奇异模替代本征模统一描述动力学结构。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: Koopman学习中要求有限模型将字典映射到自身是不必要的人为约束，放松该约束后需要新的数学对象（奇异模）来刻画可观测量与其投影未来之间的关系。

主要方法: 基于Ψ(X)K ≈ Φ(Y)构建Petrov-Galerkin回归，采用不同试验与测试字典；将DMD、EDMD、SINDy、Koopman回归等统一为该框架的变体；利用奇异模识别可观测量组合、投影未来及空间耦合强度；推导极限投影算子并证明L²收敛性。

实验 / 验证: 在混沌映射、双涡旋、俯仰翼型尾流和Lorenz-63系统上进行测试，与DeepONets、Fourier神经算子和储备池计算进行对比。

主要结果: PRONE在所有测试案例中均优于DeepONets、Fourier神经算子和储备池计算，且使用参数量大幅减少。

结论: 通过一次提升和一次回归，奇异结构能够统一揭示动力学系统的统计特征、输运、预测和维度，PRONE提供了一个简洁高效的通用框架。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 数据驱动的动力学研究通常关注如何线性化非线性系统。我们转而提出：哪些可观测量应该被推进，以及它们的未来应该存在于何处？这引出了非线性演化的Petrov回归（PRONE），一种基于Ψ(X)K ≈ Φ(Y)的Petrov-Galerkin回归框架，具有不同的试验字典和测试字典。在此形式下，DMD、EDMD、SINDy、Koopman回归、稀疏回归和低秩回归成为同一构造的变体：不同的字典、权重和约束。我们保留了Koopman学习的线性代数框架，但放弃了有限模型将字典映射到自身的人为要求。由于这种不对称性，本征模不再是正确的分析对象。取而代之，我们使用奇异模，它能够识别数据所捕捉的可观测量组合、它们的投影未来，以及两个空间之间的耦合强度。我们确定了极限投影算子，并证明了所得非线性预测器的L²收敛性。我们给出了来自混沌映射、双涡旋、俯仰翼型尾流和Lorenz-63的实例，其中PRONE以远少于DeepONets、Fourier神经算子和储备池计算的参数量取得了更优的性能。这些实例传达了同样的信息：提升一次，回归一次，让奇异结构揭示统计特征、输运、预测和维度。

英文原始摘要: Data-driven dynamics often asks how to linearize a nonlinear system. We ask instead: which observables should be advanced, and where should their futures live? This leads to Petrov Regression Of Nonlinear Evolution (PRONE), a Petrov--Galerkin regression framework based on $
  Ψ(\mathbf{X})K \approx Φ(\mathbf{Y}), $ with distinct trial and test dictionaries. In this form, DMD, EDMD, SINDy, Koopman regression, sparse regression, and low-rank regression become variants of one construction: different dictionaries, weights, and constraints. We keep the linear algebra of Koopman learning, but drop the artificial requirement that a finite model map a dictionary into itself. With this asymmetry, eigenmodes are no longer the right objects. Instead, we use singular modes, which identify the observable combinations captured by the data, their projected futures, and the strength of the coupling between the two spaces. We identify the limiting projected operator and prove $L^2$ convergence of the resulting nonlinear predictor. We give examples from chaotic maps, the double gyre, a pitching-airfoil wake, and Lorenz--63, where PRONE outperforms DeepONets, Fourier neural operators, and reservoir computers with considerably fewer parameters. These examples show the same message: lift once, regress once, and let the singular structure reveal statistics, transport, prediction, and dimension.

</details>


### [57] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459v1)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI: 

免费全文: [arXiv abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459v1), [arXiv pdf](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.27459v1), [Google Scholar search](https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=Operator+Learning+for+Cubic+Nonlinear+Schr%C3%B6dinger+Equation+on+Periodic+Domains++free+full+text+pdf)

一句话结论: 提出几何条件化FNO学习二维环上NLS方程解算子，验证长时预测精度提升。

研究问题: 如何在神经算子中显式编码几何信息（纵横比），以准确捕捉有理与无理二维环面上立方NLS方程不同的高频级联与索博列夫范数行为。

主要创新点: 提出几何条件化傅里叶神经算子，将纵横比参数$ω^2$作为显式输入，使模型能够区分不同共振结构下的解动力学。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 二维平坦环面的纵横比控制傅里叶共振结构，有理与无理几何表现出不同的高频能量级联行为，传统神经算子缺乏对此几何依赖性的显式建模。

主要方法: 构建几何条件化FNO，输入为解的实部、虚部及$ω^2$参数，训练逼近立方散焦NLS方程的单步解算子，参考数据由傅里叶伪谱方法生成。

实验 / 验证: 在随机相位初始数据生成的未见轨迹上评估模型；开展消融研究，考察保留傅里叶模式数、激活函数、傅里叶层深度及几何条件化的影响。

主要结果: 学习算子在两种环面上均捕捉主要动力学，重现了有理环面更强的$H^2$增长与无理环面更受限的行为；包含$ω^2$显著提升长时间预测精度，尤其对有理几何。

结论: 显式几何条件化有助于神经算子学习非线性色散PDE中的谱转移现象，几何感知设计对长时间预测精度有实质性贡献。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们考虑在具有不同纵横比的二维平坦环面上的立方非线性薛定谔（NLS）方程。在此表述中，纵横比的选择控制了傅里叶共振结构，因此有理几何和无理几何可以表现出不同的高频级联行为。我们提出了一种用于立方散焦NLS方程的几何条件化傅里叶神经算子（FNO），其输入包括解的实部和虚部以及纵横比参数$ω^2$。该模型被训练以逼近单步解算子，并在使用傅里叶伪谱方法从随机相位初始数据生成的未见轨迹上进行评估。我们的数值实验表明，学习到的算子能够捕捉两种环面上的主要解动力学，并重现两种几何不同的索博列夫范数行为，其中有理环面上表现出更强的$H^2$增长，无理环面上行为更为受限，这与\cite{hrabski2021energy}的发现一致。我们进行了消融研究，以检验保留的傅里叶模式、激活函数、傅里叶层深度和显式几何条件化的作用。结果表明，包含$ω^2$提高了长时间预测精度，尤其对于有理几何，并支持使用几何感知神经算子来学习非线性色散偏微分方程中的谱转移现象。

英文原始摘要: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

中文摘要: 注意力机制使世界模型能够以整个历史为条件，提供促进长程预测的长期记忆。虽然我们的伴随论文中的局部Onsager-Machlup理论假设了时间局域的预测作用，我们研究了该局域性成立的条件。我们推导了由于注意力诱导的记忆而变为非马尔可夫的潜动力学的预测路径测度，证明该测度是隐线性马尔可夫增广的投影。消除辅助场后得到非局部Onsager-Machlup作用，其中记忆以非局部二次型而非力的形式出现。这些核是完全单调的，且精确匹配具有有限弛豫谱的隐马尔可夫嵌入；否则，动力学保持本质上的非局部性。通过按尺度分离参数$ε=τ_{\text{mem}}/τ_{\text{dyn}}$展开作用，我们证明首阶项恢复了伴随论文中的局部作用，从而将局域性确立为非局部理论的短记忆极限。我们逐项验证了该展开的可逆部分与一个精确可解的向量线性模型的一致性。

英文原始摘要: Attention enables a world model to condition on its entire history, providing long-term memory that facilitates long-range predictions. While the local Onsager--Machlup theory in our companion paper assumes a temporally local predictive action, we investigate the conditions under which this locality holds. We derive the predictive path measure for latent dynamics that become non-Markovian due to attention-induced memory, demonstrating that this measure is the projection of a hidden linear Markov augmentation. Eliminating the auxiliary field results in a nonlocal Onsager--Machlup action, where memory manifests as a nonlocal quadratic form rather than a force. These kernels are completely monotone and exactly match a hidden Markov embedding with a finite relaxation spectrum; otherwise, the dynamics remain fundamentally nonlocal. By expanding the action in terms of the scale-separation parameter $ε=τ_{\text{mem}}/τ_{\text{dyn}}$, we show that the leading order recovers the local action of the companion paper, establishing locality as the short-memory limit of a nonlocal theory. We verify the reversible sector of this expansion term by term against an exactly solvable vector linear model.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 稀疏且含噪的DMRS观测以及跨场景的配置变化使得传统信道估计方法性能下降，亟需一种能自适应噪声水平并利用学习先验的鲁棒估计方案。

主要方法: 将DMRS辅助信道估计建模为稀疏线性逆问题，通过值-零空间分解将值空间分量（受测量约束）与零空间分量（未观测）分离；零空间分量通过学习的扩散先验重建；在反向扩散过程中引入噪声自适应后验校正，校正系数和残差采样方差按噪声水平联合标定；设计面向复值OFDM信道网格的条件U-Net去噪器，实虚部分离为独立特征通道且仅沿子载波维度下采样。

实验 / 验证: 基于5G NR TDL和CDL信道模型进行仿真，在不同信噪比、DMRS配置、多普勒频移及训练-测试分布不匹配条件下，与传统估计器和基于扩散的后验采样方法对比NMSE性能。

主要结果: DANCE在所有测试条件下均持续取得低于传统估计器和基于扩散的后验采样方法的归一化均方误差（NMSE），展现出良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

结论: DANCE通过值-零空间分解与噪声自适应后验校正的扩散先验重建，有效解决了稀疏含噪DMRS场景下OFDM信道估计问题，在多种场景下优于现有方法。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 当解调参考信号（DMRS）观测稀疏且含噪，以及DMRS配置在不同部署场景下变化时，正交频分复用（OFDM）系统中的精确信道估计仍具有挑战性。本文提出了DANCE（基于扩散的噪声自适应零空间信道估计），一种用于OFDM系统的基于扩散模型的信道估计器。我们将DMRS辅助的信道估计建模为一个稀疏线性逆问题，其测量算子由导频模式诱导。由此产生的值-零空间分解将测量约束的值空间分量与未观测的零空间分量分离，后者通过学习的扩散先验进行重建。为避免将含噪导频样本直接作为精确约束，DANCE在反向扩散过程中引入了噪声自适应后验校正。校正系数和残差采样方差根据观测噪声水平进行联合标定，从而在减少导频噪声注入的同时保留有用的测量信息。我们进一步设计了一个面向复值OFDM信道网格的条件U-Net去噪器，其中实部和虚部表示为独立的特征通道，并且仅在子载波维度上进行下采样。基于5G NR抽头延迟线（TDL）和簇延迟线（CDL）信道模型的仿真表明，在不同信噪比、DMRS配置、多普勒频移以及训练-测试分布不匹配条件下，DANCE的归一化均方误差（NMSE）始终低于传统估计器和基于扩散的后验采样方法。

英文原始摘要: Accurate channel estimation in orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) systems remains challenging when demodulation reference signal (DMRS) observations are sparse and noisy, and when DMRS configurations vary across deployment scenarios. This paper proposes DANCE (Diffusion-based Noise-Adaptive Null-space Channel Estimation), a diffusion-based channel estimator for OFDM systems. We formulate DMRS-aided channel estimation as a sparse linear inverse problem whose measurement operator is induced by the pilot pattern. The resulting range-null space decomposition separates the measurement-constrained range-space component from the unobserved null-space component, which is reconstructed through a learned diffusion prior. To avoid directly imposing noisy pilot samples as exact constraints, DANCE introduces a noise-adaptive posterior correction into the reverse diffusion process. The correction coefficient and the residual sampling variance are jointly calibrated according to the observation noise level, thereby reducing pilot-noise injection while retaining useful measurement information. We further design a conditional U-Net denoiser for complex-valued OFDM channel grids, where the real and imaginary components are represented as separate feature channels and downsampling is performed only along the subcarrier dimension. Simulations based on 5G NR tapped delay line (TDL) and clustered delay line (CDL) channel models show that DANCE achieves consistently lower normalized mean squared error (NMSE) than conventional estimators and diffusion-based posterior sampling methods under different signal-to-noise ratios, DMRS configurations, Doppler frequency shifts, and train-test distribution mismatches.

</details>

中文摘要: 绘制II型超新星（SNe II）的爆炸性质与其前身星性质之间的关系图，可以为理解大质量恒星最终演化阶段提供强有力的约束。与单颗红超巨星（RSG）爆炸相关的IIP型超新星，最近被发现需要某种形式的星周物质（CSM）相互作用，才能重现其光变曲线中常见的快速上升至平台期的特征。在本工作中，我们展示了IIP型超新星SN 2020bij的观测和分析结果，其特征是缓慢上升至平台期以及高膨胀速度。我们从文献中鉴定了另外四个具有类似缓慢上升光变曲线的IIP型超新星（ASASSN-14kg、SN 2018fif、SN 2021yja和SN 2023axu），并发现它们也表现出高膨胀速度。使用解析模型和数值模型，这五个事件均可以用弱至无CSM相互作用来解释。因此，我们提出这些事件构成了IIP型超新星的一个新子类，可能与相对受限的CSM相关。对未来IIP型超新星进行早期密集的光度覆盖以及早期光谱观测，将进一步描绘这一子类及其物理性质。理解此类罕见事件可能是约束红超巨星晚期质量损失多样性的关键。

英文原始摘要: Mapping how the explosion properties of Type II supernovae (SNe II) relate to the properties of their progenitors can provide strong constraints for understanding the final evolutionary stages of massive stars. Type IIP SNe, linked to the explosions of single red super-giant (RSG) stars, have recently been found to require some form of interaction with circumstellar material (CSM) to reproduce the rapid rise to the plateau often seen in their light curves. In this work, we present observations and analysis of the Type IIP SN 2020bij, characterized by a slow rise to its plateau as well as high expansion velocities. We identify four other SNe IIP from the literature (ASASSN-14kg, SN 2018fif, SN 2021yja and SN 2023axu) with similarly slowly rising light curves and find that they also show high expansion velocities. Using both analytical and numerical models, all five events can be explained with weak to no CSM interaction. We therefore propose that these events constitute a new subclass of Type IIP SNe which could be associated with relatively confined CSM. Early and dense photometric coverage of future SNe IIP together with early spectroscopic observations will further map this subclass and its physical properties. Understanding such rare events could be key to constraining the diversity of late-stage mass-loss in RSGs.

</details>


### [61] [Brownian motion in Minkowski normed spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00800v1)
*Shin-ichi Ohta,Marco Rehmeier,Kohei Suzuki*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊: 

DOI:

一句话结论: 首次在芬斯勒空间中构造出与非线性热方程相关的随机过程。

研究问题: 如何在芬斯勒几何框架（具体为闵可夫斯基赋范空间）下，构造与非线性热方程相关联的随机过程。

主要创新点: 通过求解具有奇异系数的McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程，首次在芬斯勒空间中构造出与非线性热方程对应的非线性马尔可夫过程，并证明了其路径唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 将从标准热核构造标准布朗运动的经典方法，自然扩展到具有非对称范数的闵可夫斯基赋范空间及非线性芬斯勒热方程的设定中。

主要方法: 将目标随机过程构造为奇异McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的解，使其一维时间边际密度由非线性芬斯勒热方程的基本解给出，并对其进行路径唯一性分析。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论研究，不涉及实证实验，主要进行严格的数学推导与证明。

主要结果: 成功证明了该随机微分方程的解是逐点唯一的（即概率强解），即使在亚临界区域之外系数具有奇异性。

结论: 该研究成功定义并构造了闵可夫斯基赋范空间中的布朗运动，填补了芬斯勒空间中非线性热方程相关随机过程构造的空白。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 闵可夫斯基赋范空间是配备了（可能不对称的）一致凸且光滑范数的欧几里得空间，构成了一类特殊的芬斯勒流形。我们构建了一个随机过程，其一维时间边际密度由闵可夫斯基赋范空间中非线性芬斯勒热方程的基本解给出。该过程被构造为奇异McKean-Vlasov随机微分方程的解，并构成了McKean意义下的非线性马尔可夫过程。此外，我们证明了该随机微分方程的解是逐点唯一的，因而是概率强解，尽管该方程在亚临界区域之外具有奇异系数。由于我们的构造是从标准热核构造标准布朗运动的自然扩展，我们将此过程称为闵可夫斯基赋范空间中的布朗运动。据我们所知，这是首次在芬斯勒空间中构造与非线性热方程相关的随机过程。

英文原始摘要: A Minkowski normed space is the Euclidean space equipped with a (possibly asymmetric) uniformly convex and smooth norm, forming a particular class of Finsler manifolds. We construct a stochastic process with one-dimensional time marginal densities given by the fundamental solution to the nonlinear Finsler heat equation in Minkowski normed spaces. This process is constructed as a solution to a singular McKean--Vlasov stochastic differential equation and constitutes a nonlinear Markov process in the sense of McKean. Furthermore, we show that solutions to this stochastic differential equation are pathwise unique, and thus probabilistically strong solutions, though the equation has singular coefficients beyond the subcritical regime. Since our construction is a natural extension of the construction of standard Brownian motion from the standard heat kernel, we call this process \emph{Brownian motion in Minkowski normed spaces.} To the best of our knowledge, this is the first construction of stochastic processes associated with nonlinear heat equation in Finslerian spaces.

</details>

中文摘要: 自主移动机器人（AMR）在仓库中的电池充电是一个关键的操作挑战，对订单处理时间和吞吐量有重大影响。在本研究中，我们解决了随机订单到达下的动态AMR充电问题，其中机器人必须学习最优充电决策。传统的固定规则启发式方法在动态环境中通常表现次优，且未能考虑多AMR协调，导致严重的资源低效。为克服这些局限性，我们提出了一种基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习（DRL）框架，专为配备固定充电站的多区块仓库设计。我们的模型动态学习两个关键决策：充电站选择和最优充电时长，并明确考虑了充电站的预期排队时间。大量数值实验将所提出的模型与最先进的DRL和传统启发式方法进行了基准比较。结果表明，与最强基线相比，我们的PPO框架将订单完成率提高了多达6%，同时显著减少了用于充电操作的总时间。此外，我们验证了模型在不同仓库配置和随机到达率下的鲁棒性。最后，我们对学习到的DRL策略进行了解释，为其相对于标准基准的优越性提供了有价值的运营洞察。

英文原始摘要: Battery charging of Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) in warehouses is a critical operational challenge that heavily impacts both order processing times and throughput. In this study, we address the dynamic AMR charging problem under stochastic order arrivals, where robots must learn optimal charging decisions. Traditional fixed-rule heuristics often prove suboptimal in dynamic environments and fail to account for multi-AMR coordination, leading to severe resource inefficiencies. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) framework designed for multi-block warehouses with fixed charging stations. Our model dynamically learns two key decisions: charging station selection and optimal charging duration, explicitly accounting for anticipated queuing times at the stations. Extensive numerical experiments benchmark the proposed model against state-of-the-art DRL and traditional heuristic approaches. Results demonstrate that our PPO framework increases order-completion rates by up to 6\% compared to the strongest baseline, while significantly reducing the total time dedicated to recharging operations. Furthermore, we validate the model's robustness across diverse warehouse configurations and stochastic arrival rates. Finally, we interpret the learned DRL policy, offering valuable operational insights into its superiority over standard benchmarks.

</details>

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们提出了一种离线强化学习（RL）框架，用于优化仓库履行环境中的SLAM吞吐量控制。SLAM（扫描/贴标/应用/清单）吞吐量直接影响系统拥塞和运营效率。我们基于RL的控制方法动态推荐SLAM吞吐量设置，通过智能调节节流行为自适应地平衡吞吐量最大化与下游稳定性。我们引入了历史信息状态表示、面向延迟影响控制的动作空间抽象，以及同时捕获上游和下游运营指标的奖励函数。我们的方法与算法无关，可在统一架构下集成多种离线RL方法。我们使用三种最先进的离线RL算法实例化了该框架，并利用来自大规模仓库的去标识化历史运营日志离线训练模型。策略性能通过综合多方法策略进行评估，包括无模型方法（通过回归模型进行即时奖励估计和长时域Fitted Q Evaluation（FQE）），以及基于模型的Deep Koopman动力学评估。实验结果表明，CQL策略持续优于其他方案，系统健康度提升22.97%，平均节流时长减少3.18%。这些发现证明了离线RL在安全且可扩展的仓库吞吐量控制优化方面的潜力。

英文原始摘要: We present an offline reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimizing SLAM throughput control in a warehouse fulfillment environment. SLAM (Scan/Label/Apply/Manifest) throughput directly influences system congestion and operational efficiency. Our RL-based control approach dynamically recommends SLAM throughput settings that adaptively balance throughput maximization with downstream stability through intelligent adjustment of throttling behavior. We include a history-informed state representation, action space abstraction for delayed-impact control, and a reward function that captures both upstream and downstream operational metrics. Our approach is algorithm-agnostic, enabling integration of multiple offline RL methods under a unified architecture. We instantiate our framework with three state-of-the-art offline RL algorithms, and trained the models offline using de-identified historical operational logs from a large-scale warehouse. Policy performance is evaluated using a comprehensive multi-method strategy. These include model-free approaches including immediate reward estimation via regression models and long-horizon Fitted Q Evaluation (FQE), as well as model-based Deep Koopman dynamics evaluation. Empirical results reveal that the CQL policy consistently outperforms alternatives, improving system health by 22.97% and reducing average throttling duration by 3.18%. These findings demonstrate the potential of offline RL for safe and scalable warehouse throughput control optimization.

</details>

主要创新点: 将液态神经网络作为潜在动力学模型，并通过解耦将潜在状态分解为退化分量与工况分量，分别施加针对性损失函数进行监督。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 预测性维护模型不仅需要预测精度，还需要内部状态能够暴露可解释的退化过程，以弥合预测性能与可检查健康状态建模之间的差距。

主要方法: 模型将历史窗口编码为潜在状态，使用液态转移模型演化状态并解码未来传感器观测；潜在状态被因子化为退化分量和工况分量，退化分量由RUL、单调风险和潜在一致性损失监督，工况分量由工况预测和去相关损失约束以防止泄漏。

实验 / 验证: 在C-MAPSS基准FD001-FD004数据集上进行实验，与GRU基线对比传感器预测RMSE、退化状态时间一致性（Spearman相关）及直接RUL回归性能。

主要结果: 整体传感器预测RMSE从GRU的0.2438降至0.2266，多工况子集FD002和FD004提升最大；退化状态速度Spearman相关达0.5960；但直接RUL回归仍弱于GRU基线。

结论: 液态潜在动力学模型能有效桥接预测性维护中的传感器预测与可解释健康状态建模，作为退化动力学的可解释世界模型具有优势，但作为校准寿命回归器尚有不足。

局限 / 后续问题: 在直接剩余使用寿命回归任务上仍弱于GRU基线，表明该表征更适合作为可解释世界模型而非校准的寿命回归器；泛化性仅在C-MAPSS基准上验证。

中文摘要: 用于预测性维护的多变量时间序列模型通常以点预测精度作为评估标准，但其内部状态很少能够呈现出连贯的退化过程。我们将液态神经网络作为潜在动力学模型，应用于C-MAPSS基准上的航空发动机健康监测。所提模型将历史窗口编码为潜在状态，通过液态转移模型演化该状态，并解码未来传感器观测值。为将健康演化与工况变化分离，潜在状态被分解为退化分量和工况分量。剩余使用寿命、单调风险及潜在一致性损失监督退化分量，而工况预测与去相关损失则抑制工况信息的泄漏。在FD001至FD004数据集上，完整的解耦模型将整体传感器预测RMSE从GRU基线的0.2438提升至0.2266，在多工况子集FD002和FD004上提升最为显著。学习到的退化状态也形成了更清晰的时间退化轴，平均状态速度Spearman相关系数达到0.5960。然而，直接的剩余使用寿命回归任务中GRU基线表现更优，表明所提表征目前更适合作为退化动力学的可解释世界模型，而非校准的寿命回归器。这些结果表明，液态潜在动力学能够弥合预测性维护预测与可检查健康状态建模之间的差距。

英文原始摘要: Multivariate time-series models for prognostics are often evaluated by point prediction accuracy, yet their internal states rarely expose a coherent degradation process. We study liquid neural networks as latent dynamics models for aircraft engine health monitoring on the C-MAPSS benchmark. The proposed model encodes a history window into a latent state, evolves that state with a liquid transition model, and decodes future sensor observations. To separate health evolution from operating-condition variation, the latent state is factorized into degradation and condition components. Remaining useful life, monotonic risk, and latent-consistency losses supervise the degradation component, while condition prediction and decorrelation losses discourage operating-condition leakage. Across FD001--FD004, the full disentangled model improves overall sensor forecasting RMSE from 0.2438 for a GRU baseline to 0.2266, with the largest gains on the multi-condition subsets FD002 and FD004. The learned degradation state also forms a clearer temporal degradation axis, reaching an average state-speed Spearman correlation of 0.5960. Direct remaining-useful-life regression remains stronger for the GRU baseline, indicating that the proposed representation is currently more effective as an interpretable world model for degradation dynamics than as a calibrated lifetime regressor. These results suggest that liquid latent dynamics can bridge predictive maintenance forecasting and inspectable health-state modeling.

</details>


### [65] [The local product condition implies cutoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05345v1)
*Francesco Pedrotti,Justin Salez*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊:

一句话结论: 用非平衡精细化的Poincaré常数γ⋆刻画马尔可夫过程混合窗口宽度为O(1/γ⋆)。

研究问题: 混合时间理论中经典猜想认为Poincaré常数与混合时间的乘积发散即可保证cutoff，但该猜想是错误的，需要找到正确的刻画条件。

主要创新点: 引入Poincaré常数的非平衡精细化版本γ⋆替代原始Poincaré常数，并在χ²散度分析中采用随时间演化的参考测度而非固定的平衡分布。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 经典cutoff猜想直觉合理但已被证明错误，需要找到一个正确且普适的条件来刻画马尔可夫过程的混合窗口宽度。

主要方法: 用χ²散度替代总变差距离，关键创新在于参考测度随时间演化而非固定为平衡律；对确定性初始化情形利用Bakry-Émery曲率κ给出γ⋆的下界。

实验 / 验证: 本文为纯理论工作，不涉及数值实验。

主要结果: 证明任意马尔可夫过程的混合窗口宽度为O(1/γ⋆)，该估计精确且在标准正则性假设下普适：适用于有限/无限状态空间、任意初始条件，无需可逆性或链式法则；确定性初始化下γ⋆≥κ。

结论: 将Poincaré常数替换为非平衡精细化版本γ⋆后，经典cutoff猜想变为正确，结果普适且证明简短自包含。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 在混合时间理论中，一个著名的错误猜想预测，当马尔可夫过程的Poincaré常数与混合时间的乘积发散时，该马尔可夫过程序列就呈现cutoff现象。我们证明，一旦将Poincaré常数γ替换为其自然的非平衡精细版本（记为γ⋆），该论断就变为正确的。更精确地说，我们证明任意马尔可夫过程的混合窗口宽度为O(1/γ⋆)。该估计是精确的，且在标准正则性假设下是普适的：它适用于有限和无限状态空间以及任意初始条件，且不需要可逆性或任何类型的链式法则。此外，对于确定性初始化，我们证明γ⋆≥κ，其中κ是Bakry-Émery曲率，使得我们的结果具有广泛的适用性。最后，我们的证明简短且自包含：我们仅遵循用更易处理的χ²散度代替总变差距离的经典思路，但关键创新在于参考测度随时间演化，而非固定为平衡分布。

英文原始摘要: In the theory of mixing times, a famously wrong conjecture predicts that a sequence of Markov processes exhibits cutoff as soon as the product of their Poincaré constant and mixing time diverges. We prove that this statement becomes correct once the Poincaré constant $γ$ is replaced with its natural non-equilibrium refinement, which we denote by $γ_\star$. More precisely, we show that the width of the mixing window of any Markov process is $O(1/γ_\star)$. This estimate is sharp, and universal up to standard regularity assumptions: it holds on finite and infinite state spaces and from any initial condition, and it does not require reversibility, nor any kind of a chain rule. In addition, for deterministic initialization we show that $γ_\star\geκ$, where $κ$ is the Bakry-Émery curvature, making our result broadly applicable. Finally, our proof is short and self-contained: we simply follow the classical idea of replacing the total variation distance by the more tractable $χ^2$-divergence, but with the crucial novelty that the reference measure evolves in time, instead of being the equilibrium law.

</details>

研究动机: 现有去中心化联邦学习的典型实验场景忽略了通信网络固有的时间和结构不均匀性，可能导致对收敛速度的错误评估。

主要方法: 通过建立去中心化联邦学习与时态网络上懒惰随机游走扩散过程的动力学映射，推导并分析模型参数的聚合行为。

实验 / 验证: 对现实世界的时态网络进行了分析，以评估时间和结构不均匀性对扩散过程及模型收敛的实际影响。

主要结果: 典型实验场景因忽略网络不均匀性而产生了不切实际的快速收敛；在真实时态网络中，不均匀性通常会极大地减缓扩散与收敛。

结论: 去中心化联邦学习的收敛速度受网络时间和结构不均匀性的显著制约，现有典型实验设置高估了收敛效率。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 基于点对点通信的去中心化联邦学习日益被提出用于机器学习模型的设备端训练，有望实现隐私保护、通信高效的训练过程且无单点故障风险。然而，在这种完全去中心化的环境中，结构和时间不均匀性的作用仍不甚了解。在此，我们研究了在聚合期间对模型参数进行局部平均时它们的影响。我们表明，去中心化联邦学习过程在早期和后期的平稳极限阶段，都与时态网络上懒惰随机游走扩散过程的动力学相同。基于这种映射，我们证明了去中心化联邦学习中使用的典型实验场景会导致不切实际的快速收敛，因为其忽略了通信网络固有的时间和结构不均匀性。我们分析了现实世界的时态网络，发现不均匀性通常会极大地减缓扩散过程，从而减缓收敛过程。

英文原始摘要: Decentralised federated learning, based on peer-to-peer communication, is increasingly proposed for on-device training of machine learning models, promising a privacy-preserving, communication-efficient training process with no risk of single-point failure. However, the role of structural and temporal inhomogeneities in such fully decentralised settings remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate their effects when model parameters are locally averaged during aggregation. We show that the decentralised federated learning process is governed, both in the early phase and the late, stationary limit, by the same dynamics as a lazy random-walk diffusion process on temporal networks. Based on this mapping, we demonstrate that the typical experimental scenario used in decentralised federated learning leads to unrealistically rapid convergence because of ignoring the temporal and structural inhomogeneities inherent in the communication network. We analyse real-world temporal networks and find that inhomogeneities most often dramatically slow down diffusion, hence the convergence process.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 非言语发声承载重要的对话和情感信息，但现有ASR系统普遍忽略这些信息；同时NV标注的稀疏性和长尾分布使罕见类别建模极为困难。

主要方法: 三种策略：（1）两阶段课程学习，先映射所有NV事件到通用token再微调目标类别；（2）从高资源事件向罕见事件进行跨token知识迁移；（3）语音转换增强配合类别平衡。

主要结果: 实验表明利用不同发声事件间的共享声学结构可显著提升罕见类别检测效果，同时不损害词汇ASR质量。

结论: 共享声学结构可有效改善低资源非言语发声识别，尤其是罕见类别，且不影响词汇识别性能。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 现代自动语音识别（ASR）系统在转录词汇内容方面表现出色，但常常省略非言语发声（NVs），如笑声、呼吸声、咳嗽声和哭声，这些声音承载着对话和情感信息。在ASR中对NVs进行建模具有挑战性，因为NV标注稀疏且呈高度长尾分布，呼吸和笑声等常见类别主导着哭泣和咳嗽等罕见事件。我们研究了三种以数据为中心的策略来改善低资源NV识别：（1）两阶段课程学习，首先将所有NV事件映射到一个通用token，然后在目标类别上进行微调；（2）从高资源事件（如笑声和呼吸）向罕见事件（如哭泣）进行跨token迁移；（3）语音转换增强与类别平衡。实验表明，可以利用不同发声事件之间的共享声学结构来提升罕见类别的检测效果，同时保持词汇ASR质量。

英文原始摘要: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems excel at transcribing lexical content but often omit nonverbal vocalizations (NVs), such as laughter, breaths, coughs, and cries, that carry conversational and affective information. Modeling NVs in ASR is challenging because NV annotations are sparse and highly long-tailed, with frequent categories such as breaths and laughter dominating rarer events such as cries and coughs. We study three data-centric strategies for improving low-resource NV recognition: (1) a two-stage curriculum that first maps all NV events to a generic token and then fine-tunes on target categories; (2) inter-token transfer from high-resource events, such as laughter and breath, to rare events, such as crying; and (3) voice-conversion augmentation with class balancing. Experiments show that shared acoustic structure across vocal events can be exploited to improve rare-category detection while preserving lexical ASR quality.

</details>

主要创新点: 基于预训练编码器集合的模块化基础模型，结合门控机制动态选择编码器、共享潜空间投影与Transformer自注意力聚合，实现条件计算与自适应模型组合。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 工程数字孪生和PHM系统需要从异构、非平稳时序数据中提取可操作信息，但现有方法难以满足跨任务迁移、数据效率和可扩展集成的需求。

主要方法: 在异构数据集上进行自监督预训练学习任务无关表征；引入门控机制动态选择目标数据集相关编码器；通过共享潜空间投影和Transformer自注意力模块聚合多编码器表征；使用轻量级任务特定头支持下游任务，适配时冻结预训练编码器。

实验 / 验证: 开展了大量消融实验验证各组件互补性；在ETT基准上评估插补、长期预测和小样本学习等任务；在水轮发电机转子温度虚拟感知的实际工业场景中进行案例研究。

主要结果: ETT基准上跨任务取得竞争性性能；消融实验证明自监督预训练、编码器选择、表征对齐和自适应聚合各自的互补贡献；工业案例验证了实际适用性。

结论: 所提出的模块化基础模型能够有效学习可迁移的时序表征，在多个PHM下游任务中表现竞争性，并在实际工业场景中具有实用价值。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 工程数字孪生与故障预测与健康管理（PHM）系统依赖鲁棒的感知模块从异构和非平稳时间序列数据中提取可操作信息。然而，现有方法大多局限于特定任务、数据需求量大，且难以集成到可扩展的监测与决策流程中。此外，纯数据驱动模型在不同运行条件下往往缺乏鲁棒性和可迁移性。为应对这些挑战，本文提出了一种基于预训练表征编码器集合的模块化时间序列感知基础模型。该框架利用自监督学习在异构数据集上学习可迁移的、与任务无关的表征，这些表征可在多个PHM任务中复用。引入门控机制为给定目标数据集动态选择相关编码器，实现条件计算和自适应模型组合。所选表征被投影到共享潜空间，并使用基于Transformer的自注意力模块进行聚合，该模块显式建模编码器间的交互。所得架构通过轻量级任务特定头支持多种下游任务，包括插补、长期预测和小样本学习，同时在适配过程中保持预训练编码器冻结。大量消融实验证明了自监督预训练、编码器选择、表征对齐和自适应聚合的互补作用。在ETT基准上的实验结果展示了跨任务的竞争性性能，而水轮发电机转子温度虚拟感知的实际工业案例研究突出了该方法的实用价值。

英文原始摘要: Engineering Digital Twins and Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) systems rely on robust perception modules to extract actionable information from heterogeneous and non-stationary time-series data. However, most existing approaches remain task-specific, data-hungry, and difficult to integrate into scalable monitoring and decision-making pipelines. Moreover, purely data-driven models often lack robustness and transferability across varying operating conditions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a modular foundation model for time-series perception based on a collection of pretrained representation encoders. The framework leverages self-supervised learning on heterogeneous datasets to learn transferable and task-agnostic representations, which can be reused across multiple PHM tasks. A gating mechanism is introduced to dynamically select relevant encoders for a given target dataset, enabling conditional computation and adaptive model composition. The selected representations are projected into a shared latent space and aggregated using a Transformer-based self-attention module that explicitly models cross-encoder interactions. The resulting architecture supports multiple downstream tasks, including imputation, long-term forecasting, and few-shot learning, through lightweight task-specific heads, while keeping pretrained encoders frozen during adaptation. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate the complementary roles of self-supervised pretraining, encoder selection, representation alignment, and adaptive aggregation. Experimental results on the ETT benchmark show competitive performance across tasks, while a real-world industrial case study on virtual sensing for hydro-generator rotor temperature highlights the practical relevance of the approach.

</details>

*Sayan Banerjee,Amarjit Budhiraja,Peter Rudzis*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊:

一句话结论: 弱非对称碰撞局部时间驱动的布朗粒子系统经微观Hopf-Cole变换后收敛至KPZ方程，归入KPZ普适类。

研究问题: 具有由碰撞局部时间介导的奇异非对称相互作用的双无穷布朗粒子系统的适定性、平稳分布以及平衡涨落的刻画问题。

主要创新点: 在弱非对称体制下，通过碰撞链局部化、布朗最后通道渗流估计、局部时间抵消性质与尺度适配的鞅问题相结合，证明了微观Hopf-Cole变换后计数函数收敛至乘性随机热方程（等价于KPZ方程），从而将该类奇异碰撞系统归入KPZ普适类。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: 理解具有奇异非对称碰撞动力学的布朗粒子系统是否属于KPZ普适类，填补此类奇异相互作用粒子系统在宏观涨落理论中的空白。

主要方法: 通过碰撞链将无穷系统局部化为有限粒子子系统；利用布朗最后通道渗流给出估计；利用关键的局部时间抵消性质；构造尺度适配的光滑化Hopf-Cole场的鞅问题，使其时空正则性匹配极限SHE。

实验 / 验证: 本文为理论数学研究，无数值实验；核心验证为在特定弱非对称参数选取$p_\varepsilon^{-1}-1=\exp\{\sigma\varepsilon^{1/4}\}$及i.i.d. Exp(1)初始间距下，证明扩散尺度重标后微观Hopf-Cole场在$\varepsilon\to 0$时的收敛性。

主要结果: 证明了逐路径唯一性与强存在性；识别了i.i.d. Exp(λ)乘积测度为间距过程的不变分布；在弱非对称体制下，微观Hopf-Cole变换后的计数函数收敛至乘性随机热方程（SHE），其对数收敛至KPZ方程的Hopf-Cole解。

结论: 具有非对称奇异碰撞局部时间相互作用的布朗粒子系统在弱非对称极限下属于KPZ普适类，其宏观涨落由KPZ方程描述。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 我们研究了实轴上具有由碰撞局部时间介导的奇异非对称相互作用的双无穷相互作用布朗粒子系统。粒子做布朗运动，当相邻粒子碰撞时，相关的局部时间按比例$p$和$q=1-p$进行分配。我们首先发展了该粒子系统的适定性理论，在初始构型和局部时间的自然增长假设下证明了逐路径唯一性和强存在性。我们还识别了相邻粒子间距这一无穷维过程的一族平稳分布：对每个$\lambda>0$，以i.i.d. Exp$(\lambda)$间距为分量的乘积测度是不变的。我们的主要结果涉及弱非对称体制下相关粒子计数（高度）函数的平衡涨落。取$p=p_\varepsilon$使得$p_\varepsilon^{-1}-1=\exp\{\sigma\varepsilon^{1/4}\}$，以i.i.d. Exp$(1)$随机变量初始化粒子间距，并对扩散尺度重标后的计数函数施加微观Hopf-Cole变换，我们证明了当$\varepsilon\to 0$时，其收敛到具有布朗指数初始数据的乘性随机热方程（SHE）。等价地，该极限的对数是具有双侧布朗初始数据的KPZ方程的Hopf-Cole解。证明结合了通过碰撞链对有限粒子子系统的局部化、布朗最后通道渗流估计、关键的局部时间抵消性质，以及针对Hopf-Cole场尺度适配光滑化的鞅问题，其时空正则性被调节以匹配极限SHE的正则性。所得涨落定理将这些具有非对称奇异碰撞动力学的布朗粒子系统置于KPZ普适类之中。

英文原始摘要: We study a bi-infinite system of interacting Brownian particles on the real line with singular asymmetric interactions mediated by the collision local times. Particles perform Brownian motions, and when neighboring particles collide, the associated local time is split in proportions $p$ and $q=1-p$. We first develop well-posedness theory for the particle system, proving pathwise uniqueness and strong existence under natural growth assumptions on the initial configuration and local times. We also identify a family of stationary distributions for the infinite-dimensional process of gaps between successive particles: for every $λ>0$, the product measure with i.i.d. Exp$(λ)$ gaps is invariant.
  Our main result concerns the equilibrium fluctuations of the associated particle-count (height) function in a weakly asymmetric regime. Taking $p=p_\varepsilon$ so that $p_\varepsilon^{-1}-1=\exp\{σ\varepsilon^{1/4}\}$, initializing the interparticle gaps with i.i.d. Exp$(1)$ random variables, and applying a microscopic Hopf-Cole transform to the diffusively rescaled count function, we prove convergence, as $\varepsilon \rightarrow 0$, to the multiplicative stochastic heat equation (SHE) with Brownian exponential initial data. Equivalently, the logarithm of the limit is the Hopf-Cole solution of the KPZ equation with two-sided Brownian initial data. The proof combines localization to finite particle subsystems via chains of collisions, Brownian last-passage percolation estimates, a key local time cancellation property, and a martingale problem for a scale-adapted mollification of the Hopf-Cole field, whose space-time regularity is tuned to match that of the limiting SHE. The resulting fluctuation theorem places these Brownian particle systems with asymmetric singular collision dynamics within the KPZ universality class.

</details>

*Fan Wang,Peter Benner,Jan Heiland*

主要创新点: 利用可观测量、Koopman生成元及投影算子，首次为线性时不变系统推导出Mori-Zwanzig恒等式中马尔可夫项、噪声项和记忆项的闭式表示，并将其与常数变易法的结果建立等价联系。


<details>
  <summary>更多信息</summary>

研究动机: Mori-Zwanzig形式化理论性强但计算复杂，需要一个透明的线性参考案例来揭示记忆项的物理意义，并为非线性扩展提供清晰的路径指引。

主要方法: 将动力学以可观测量和Koopman生成元表示，引入向已解析与未解析分量的投影，推导马尔可夫项、噪声项和记忆项的闭式公式，并验证其与常数变易法所得降阶动力学的一致性。

实验 / 验证: 在谐振子和波动方程上进行了解析与数值示例验证，展示降阶模型的构造过程及形式化方法的应用效果。

主要结果: 闭式公式在线性情形下精确恢复了常数变易法的降阶动力学，同时保留了算子结构；数值示例表明该方法能产生可解释的降阶模型，清晰揭示未解析变量通过历史依赖项对观测动力学的影响。

结论: 该显式表述为带记忆的降阶建模提供了透明的线性参考案例，明确了向非线性系统和更一般投影扩展所需的要素（谱滤波、数据驱动记忆近似），为后续研究奠定了基础。

局限 / 后续问题: 当前仅限于线性时不变系统，非线性系统和更一般投影的扩展尚未实现，记忆效应的数据驱动近似方法仍待进一步发展。

中文摘要: Mori-Zwanzig形式化提供了一个系统化的框架，用于在仅观测到部分状态时推导动力学系统的降阶模型，但其实际应用常受限于计算复杂性。本文针对部分可观测量下的线性时不变系统，发展了Mori-Zwanzig方程的显式表述。通过以可观测量、Koopman生成元以及向已解析和未解析分量的投影来表达动力学，我们推导出了Mori-Zwanzig恒等式中产生的马尔可夫项、噪声项和记忆项的闭式表示。对于线性情形，所得公式恢复了由常数变易法得到的降阶动力学，同时保留了Mori-Zwanzig方法的算子结构。这使该推导成为带记忆降阶建模的一个透明参考案例，并阐明了未解析变量如何通过依赖历史的项影响观测动力学。该分析还确定了向非线性系统和更一般投影扩展所需的要素，包括谱滤波和记忆效应的数据驱动近似。涉及谐振子和波动方程的解析与数值示例说明了该构造方法，并展示了如何利用该形式化获得部分观测系统的可解释降阶模型。

英文原始摘要: The Mori-Zwanzig formalism provides a systematic framework for deriving reduced-order model of dynamical systems when only part of the state is observed, but its practical use is often limited by the complexity of the resulting computations. This paper develops an explicit formulation of the Mori-Zwanzig equation for linear time-invariant systems under partially observed observables. By expressing the dynamics in terms of observables, the Koopman generator, and projections onto resolved and unresolved components, we derive closed-form representations of the Markovian, noise, and memory contributions that arise in the Mori-Zwanzig identity.
  For the linear setting, the resulting formulas recover the reduced dynamics obtained from the variation-of-constants formula while retaining the operator-based structure of the Mori-Zwanzig approach. This makes the derivation a transparent reference case for reduced-order modelling with memory and clarifies how unresolved variables influence the observed dynamics through history-dependent terms. The analysis also identifies the ingredients needed for extensions to nonlinear systems and more general projections, including spectral filtering and data-driven approximations of memory effects. Analytical and numerical examples involving the harmonic oscillator and wave equations illustrate the construction and demonstrate how the formalism can be used to obtain interpretable reduced-order models for partially observed systems.

</details>

  <summary>更多信息</summary>

主要方法: 提出物理信息傅里叶-小波Transformer，包含混合傅里叶-小波谱编码模块和基于PDE残差诊断的物理偏置自注意力机制，采用掩码物理预测与方程一致性预测进行自监督预训练，用于下一步速度场重建。

主要结果: 圆柱尾流基准上全通道NMSE为0.05875、皮尔逊相关系数0.97019，综合精度最优；流固相互作用基准上全通道NMSE为2.70×10⁻⁴，优于最强基线的4.02×10⁻⁴；尺度分离诊断显示近体、尾核和远尾等局部结构恢复更强。

结论: 所提模型在真实流动重建中实现了精度提升，同时保持了实用的精度-成本权衡。

局限 / 后续问题: 

中文摘要: 物理信息代理模型可以加速计算流体力学模拟。然而，许多现有方法在重现全局流动模式方面比局部多尺度结构更为可靠。本研究提出了一种物理信息傅里叶-小波Transformer，用于真实流动基准中的下一步速度场重建。该公式将混合傅里叶-小波谱编码与基于偏微分方程残差诊断的物理偏置自注意力机制相结合，并通过掩码物理预测和方程一致性预测进行自监督预训练。实验在两个真实基准案例上进行：圆柱尾流和流固相互作用。所有方法在统一的局部评估协议下进行评估，并与谱方法、基于Transformer的方法、算子学习和物理信息神经网络基线进行比较。在圆柱尾流基准上，所提模型实现了最佳的综合精度，全通道归一化均方误差为0.05875，全通道皮尔逊相关系数为0.97019。在流固相互作用基准上，全通道归一化均方误差最低为2.70×10⁻⁴，而最强基线为4.02×10⁻⁴。逐分量场对比和尺度分离诊断进一步表明，该模型对局部尾流结构（包括近体、尾核和远尾特征）的恢复能力更强。结果表明，该方法在保持实用性精度-成本权衡的同时，实现了真实流动重建的改进。

英文原始摘要: Physics-informed surrogate models can accelerate computational fluid dynamics simulations. However, many existing methods reproduce global flow patterns more reliably than localized multiscale structures. This study presents a physics-informed Fourier-wavelet transformer for next-step velocity-field reconstruction in real-world flow benchmarks. The proposed formulation combines hybrid Fourier-wavelet spectral encoding with physics-biased self-attention based on partial differential equation residual diagnostics. It also uses self-supervised pretraining through Masked Physics Prediction and Equation Consistency Prediction. The experiments are conducted on two real benchmark cases: cylinder-wake flow and fluid-structure interaction. All approaches are evaluated under a shared local protocol and compared with spectral, transformer-based, operator-learning, and physics-informed neural-network baselines. On the cylinder-wake benchmark, the proposed model achieves the best aggregate accuracy, with an all-channel normalized mean-squared error of 0.05875 and an all-channel Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.97019. On the fluid-structure-interaction benchmark, it gives the lowest all-channel normalized mean-squared error of $2.70 \times 10^{-4}$, compared with $4.02 \times 10^{-4}$ for the strongest baseline. Component-wise field comparisons and scale-separated diagnostics further show stronger recovery of localized wake structures, including near-body, wake-core, and far-wake features. The results demonstrate improved real-world flow reconstruction while maintaining a practical accuracy-cost tradeoff.

</details>


### [72] [A spectral-subspace-augmented POD-Galerkin method for parametrized PDEs with limited snapshot data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01534v1)
*Tianhao Hu,Zecheng Gan*

主分类: 数值计算与科学计算

来源: arxiv

期刊:
